===== M0002 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.IRFactory.transform(AstNode)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/IRFactory.java
Original range: 158-180
Documentation:

Method code:
158 |   private Node transform(AstNode node) {
159 |     Node irNode = justTransform(node);
160 |     // If we have a named function, set the position to that of the name.
161 |     if (irNode.getType() == Token.FUNCTION &&
162 |         irNode.getFirstChild().getLineno() != -1) {
163 |       irNode.setLineno(irNode.getFirstChild().getLineno());
164 |       irNode.setCharno(irNode.getFirstChild().getCharno());
165 |     } else {
166 |       if (irNode.getLineno() == -1) {
167 |         // If we didn't already set the line, then set it now.  This avoids
168 |         // cases like ParenthesizedExpression where we just return a previous
169 |         // node, but don't want the new node to get its parent's line number.
170 |         int lineno = node.getLineno();
171 |         irNode.setLineno(lineno);
172 |         int charno = position2charno(node.getAbsolutePosition());
173 |         irNode.setCharno(charno);
174 |       }
175 |     }
176 |     if (node.getJsDoc() != null) {
177 |       irNode.putProp(TMP_JSDOC_PROP, node.getJsDoc());
178 |     }
179 |     return irNode;
180 |   }

/**
   * Adds a node at the left-hand side of an expression. Unlike
   * {@link #addExpr(Node,int)}, this preserves information about the context.
   *
   * The left side of an expression is special because in the JavaScript
   * grammar, certain tokens may be parsed differently when they are at
   * the beginning of a statement. For example, "{}" is parsed as a block,
   * but "{'x': 'y'}" is parsed as an object literal.
   */
Method code:
692 |   void addLeftExpr(Node n, int minPrecedence, Context context) {
693 |     addExpr(n, minPrecedence, context);
694 |   }

===== M0014 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.FoldConstants.tryFoldGetProp(NodeTraversal,Node,Node,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/FoldConstants.java
Original range: 1599-1625
Documentation:
/**
   * Try to fold array-length. e.g [1, 2, 3].length ==> 3, [x, y].length ==> 2
   */
Method code:
1599 |   void tryFoldGetProp(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node left, Node right,
1600 |                       Node parent) {
1601 |     if (right.getType() == Token.STRING &&
1602 |         right.getString().equals("length")) {
1603 |       int knownLength = -1;
1604 |       switch (left.getType()) {
1605 |         case Token.ARRAYLIT:
1606 |           if (NodeUtil.mayHaveSideEffects(left)) {
1607 |             // Nope, can't fold this, without handling the side-effects.
1608 |             return;
1609 |           }
1610 |           knownLength = left.getChildCount();
1611 |           break;
1612 |         case Token.STRING:
1613 |           knownLength = left.getString().length();
1614 |           break;
1615 |         default:
1616 |           // Not a foldable case, forget it.
1617 |           return;
1618 |       }
1619 | 
1620 |       Preconditions.checkState(knownLength != -1);
1621 |       Node lengthNode = Node.newNumber(knownLength);
1622 |       parent.replaceChild(n, lengthNode);
1623 |       t.getCompiler().reportCodeChange();
1624 |     }
1625 |   }

===== M0017 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CodePrinter.toSource(Node,Format,boolean,int,SourceMap,Charset,boolean)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CodePrinter.java
Original range: 593-617
Documentation:
/**
   * Converts a tree to js code
   */
Method code:
593 |   private static String toSource(Node root, Format outputFormat,
594 |                                  boolean lineBreak,  int lineLengthThreshold,
595 |                                  SourceMap sourceMap,
596 |                                  Charset outputCharset,
597 |                                  boolean validation) {
598 |     boolean createSourceMap = (sourceMap != null);
599 |     CodeConsumer cp =
600 |         outputFormat == Format.COMPACT
601 |         ? new CompactCodePrinter(
602 |             lineBreak, lineLengthThreshold, createSourceMap)
603 |         : new PrettyCodePrinter(lineLengthThreshold, createSourceMap);
604 |     CodeGenerator cg =
605 |         outputFormat == Format.TYPED
606 |         ? new TypedCodeGenerator(cp, outputCharset)
607 |         : new CodeGenerator(cp, outputCharset, validation);
608 |     cg.add(root);
609 | 
610 |     String code = ((HasGetCode) cp).getCode();
611 | 
612 |     if (createSourceMap) {
613 |       cp.generateSourceMap(sourceMap);
614 |     }
615 | 
616 |     return code;
617 |   }

===== M0031 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.FoldConstants.visit(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/FoldConstants.java
Original range: 90-382
Documentation:

Method code:
90 |   public void visit(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
91 |     int type = n.getType();
92 | 
93 |     if (type == Token.BLOCK) {
94 |       tryFoldBlock(t, n, parent);
95 |       return;
96 |     }
97 | 
98 |     Node left = n.getFirstChild();
99 |     if (left == null) {
100 |       return;
101 |     }
102 | 
103 |     if (type == Token.TYPEOF && NodeUtil.isLiteralValue(left)) {
104 |       String newValue = null;
105 | 
106 |       switch (left.getType()) {
107 |         case Token.STRING:
108 |           newValue = "string";
109 |           break;
110 |         case Token.NUMBER:
111 |           newValue = "number";
112 |           break;
113 |         case Token.TRUE:
114 |         case Token.FALSE:
115 |           newValue = "boolean";
116 |           break;
117 |         case Token.NULL:
118 |         case Token.OBJECTLIT:
119 |         case Token.ARRAYLIT:
120 |           newValue = "object";
121 |           break;
122 |         case Token.NAME:
123 |           // We assume here that programs don't change the value of the
124 |           // keyword undefined to something other than the value undefined.
125 |           if ("undefined".equals(left.getString())) {
126 |             newValue = "undefined";
127 |           }
128 |           break;
129 |       }
130 | 
131 |       if (newValue != null) {
132 |         parent.replaceChild(n, Node.newString(newValue));
133 |         t.getCompiler().reportCodeChange();
134 |       }
135 | 
136 |       return;
137 |     }
138 | 
139 |     if (type == Token.NOT ||
140 |         type == Token.NEG ||
141 |         type == Token.BITNOT) {
142 |         Preconditions.checkState(n.hasOneChild());
143 | 
144 |         if (NodeUtil.isExpressionNode(parent)) {
145 |           // If the value of the NOT isn't used, then just throw
146 |           // away the operator
147 |           parent.replaceChild(n, n.removeFirstChild());
148 |           t.getCompiler().reportCodeChange();
149 |           return;
150 |         }
151 | 
152 |         // Try to mimize NOT nodes such as !(x==y) into x!=y.
153 |         if (type == Token.NOT && tryMinimizeNot(t, n, parent)) {
154 |           return;
155 |         }
156 | 
157 |         if (!NodeUtil.isLiteralValue(left)) {
158 |           return;
159 |         }
160 | 
161 |         switch (type) {
162 |           case Token.NOT:
163 |             int result = NodeUtil.getBooleanValue(left) ? Token.FALSE :
164 |                          Token.TRUE;
165 |             parent.replaceChild(n, new Node(result));
166 |             t.getCompiler().reportCodeChange();
167 |             break;
168 | 
169 |           case Token.NEG:
170 |             try {
171 |               if (left.getType() == Token.NAME) {
172 |                 if (left.getString().equals("Infinity")) {
173 |                   // "-Infinity" is valid and a literal, don't modify it.
174 |                   return;
175 |                 } else if (left.getString().equals("NaN")) {
176 |                   // "-NaN" is "NaN".
177 |                   n.removeChild(left);
178 |                   parent.replaceChild(n, left);
179 |                   t.getCompiler().reportCodeChange();
180 |                   return;
181 |                 }
182 |               }
183 | 
184 |               double negNum = -left.getDouble();
185 |               parent.replaceChild(n, Node.newNumber(negNum));
186 |               t.getCompiler().reportCodeChange();
187 |             } catch (UnsupportedOperationException ex) {
188 |               // left is not a number node, so do not replace, but warn the
189 |               // user because they can't be doing anything good
190 |               error(t, NEGATING_A_NON_NUMBER_ERROR, left);
191 |             }
192 |             break;
193 | 
194 |           case Token.BITNOT:
195 |             try {
196 |               double val = left.getDouble();
197 |               if (val >= Integer.MIN_VALUE && val <= Integer.MAX_VALUE) {
198 |                 int intVal = (int) val;
199 |                 if (intVal == val) {
200 |                   parent.replaceChild(n, Node.newNumber(~intVal));
201 |                   t.getCompiler().reportCodeChange();
202 |                 } else {
203 |                   error(t, FRACTIONAL_BITWISE_OPERAND, left);
204 |                 }
205 |               } else {
206 |                 error(t, BITWISE_OPERAND_OUT_OF_RANGE, left);
207 |               }
208 |             } catch (UnsupportedOperationException ex) {
209 |               // left is not a number node, so do not replace, but warn the
210 |               // user because they can't be doing anything good
211 |               error(t, NEGATING_A_NON_NUMBER_ERROR, left);
212 |             }
213 |             break;
214 |         }
215 |         return;
216 |     } else if (type == Token.NEW) {
217 |       if (Token.NAME == left.getType()) {
218 |         String className = left.getString();
219 |         if ("RegExp".equals(className)) {
220 |           tryFoldRegularExpressionConstructor(t, n, parent);
221 |         } else if (left.getNext() == null) {
222 |           if ("Array".equals(className)) {
223 |             tryFoldLiteralConstructor(
224 |                 t, n, parent, className, Token.ARRAYLIT);
225 |           } else if ("Object".equals(className)) {
226 |             tryFoldLiteralConstructor(
227 |                 t, n, parent, className, Token.OBJECTLIT);
228 |           }
229 |         }
230 |       }
231 |     }
232 | 
233 |     if (type == Token.EXPR_RESULT) {
234 |       tryMinimizeCondition(t, left, n);
235 |       return;
236 |     }
237 | 
238 |     if (type == Token.RETURN) {
239 |       tryReduceReturn(t, n);
240 |       return;
241 |     }
242 | 
243 |     Node right = left.getNext();
244 |     if (right == null) {
245 |       return;
246 |     }
247 | 
248 |     // TODO(johnlenz) Use type information if available to fold
249 |     // instanceof.
250 |     if (type == Token.INSTANCEOF
251 |         && NodeUtil.isLiteralValue(left)
252 |         && !NodeUtil.mayHaveSideEffects(right)) {
253 |       if (NodeUtil.isImmutableValue(left)) {
254 |         // Non-object types are never instances.
255 |         parent.replaceChild(n, new Node(Token.FALSE));
256 |         t.getCompiler().reportCodeChange();
257 |         return;
258 |       }
259 | 
260 |       if (right.getType() == Token.NAME
261 |           && "Object".equals(right.getString())) {
262 |         parent.replaceChild(n, new Node(Token.TRUE));
263 |         t.getCompiler().reportCodeChange();
264 |         return;
265 |       }
266 |     }
267 | 
268 |     if (type == Token.IF || type == Token.HOOK) {
269 |       tryMinimizeCondition(t, n.getFirstChild(), n);
270 |       boolean changes = tryFoldHookIf(t, n, parent);
271 | 
272 |       // bad cascades can occur if we run the second round
273 |       // of IF optimizations immediately
274 |       if (type == Token.IF && !changes) {
275 |         tryMinimizeIf(t, n, parent);
276 |       }
277 |       return;
278 |     }
279 | 
280 |     if (type == Token.DO) {
281 |       tryMinimizeCondition(t, NodeUtil.getConditionExpression(n), n);
282 |       tryFoldDo(t, n, parent);
283 |       return;
284 |     }
285 | 
286 |     if (type == Token.WHILE) {
287 |       tryMinimizeCondition(t, NodeUtil.getConditionExpression(n), n);
288 |       tryFoldWhile(t, n, parent);
289 |       return;
290 |     }
291 | 
292 |     if (type == Token.FOR) {
293 |       Node condition = NodeUtil.getConditionExpression(n);
294 |       if (condition != null) {
295 |         tryMinimizeCondition(t, condition, n);
296 |         // The root condition node might have changed, get it again.
297 |         condition = NodeUtil.getConditionExpression(n);
298 |         this.tryFoldForCondition(condition, n);
299 |       }
300 |         
301 |       tryFoldFor(t, n, parent);
302 |       return;
303 |     }
304 | 
305 |     if (type == Token.AND ||
306 |         type == Token.OR) {
307 |       tryFoldAndOr(t, n, left, right, parent);
308 |       return;
309 |     }
310 | 
311 |     if (type == Token.BITOR ||
312 |         type == Token.BITAND) {
313 |       tryFoldBitAndOr(t, n, left, right, parent);
314 |       return;
315 |     }
316 | 
317 |     if (type == Token.LSH ||
318 |         type == Token.RSH ||
319 |         type == Token.URSH) {
320 |       tryFoldShift(t, n, left, right, parent);
321 |       return;
322 |     }
323 | 
324 |     if (type == Token.GETPROP) {
325 |       tryFoldGetProp(t, n, left, right, parent);
326 |       return;
327 |     }
328 | 
329 |     if (type == Token.CALL) {
330 |       tryFoldStringJoin(t, n, left, right, parent);
331 |       tryFoldStringIndexOf(t, n, left, right, parent);
332 |       return;
333 |     }
334 | 
335 |     if (type == Token.ASSIGN) {
336 |       tryFoldAssign(t, n, left, right);
337 |     }
338 | 
339 |     if (!NodeUtil.isLiteralValue(left) ||
340 |         !NodeUtil.isLiteralValue(right)) {
341 | 
342 |       if (type == Token.ADD)
343 |         tryFoldLeftChildAdd(t, n, left, right, parent);
344 | 
345 |       if (type == Token.LT ||
346 |           type == Token.GT) {
347 |         tryFoldComparison(t, n, left, right, parent);
348 |       }
349 | 
350 |       return; // The subsequent ops only work if the LHS & RHS are consts
351 |     }
352 | 
353 |     if (type == Token.ADD) {
354 |       tryFoldAdd(t, n, left, right, parent);
355 |       return;
356 |     }
357 |     if (type == Token.SUB ||
358 |         type == Token.MUL ||
359 |         type == Token.DIV) {
360 |       tryFoldArithmetic(t, n, left, right, parent);
361 |       return;
362 |     }
363 | 
364 |     if (type == Token.LT ||
365 |         type == Token.GT ||
366 |         type == Token.LE ||
367 |         type == Token.GE ||
368 |         type == Token.EQ ||
369 |         type == Token.NE ||
370 |         type == Token.SHEQ ||
371 |         type == Token.SHNE) {
372 |       tryFoldComparison(t, n, left, right, parent);
373 |       return;
374 |     }
375 | 
376 |     if (type == Token.GETELEM) {
377 |       tryFoldGetElem(t, n, left, right, parent);
378 |       return;
379 |     }
380 | 
381 |     // other types aren't handled
382 |   }

/**
   * Parses the JavaScript text given by a reader.
   *
   * @param sourceName The filename.
   * @param sourceString Source code from the file.
   * @param isIdeMode Whether in IDE mode, which affects the environment.
   * @param typeRegistry The type registry.
   * @param errorReporter An error.
   * @param logger A logger.
   * @return The AST of the given text.
   * @throws IOException
   */
Method code:
80 |   public static Node parse(String sourceName,
81 |                            String sourceString,
82 |                            boolean isIdeMode,
83 |                            JSTypeRegistry typeRegistry,
84 |                            ErrorReporter errorReporter,
85 |                            Logger logger) throws IOException {
86 |     initAnnotationNames();
87 | 
88 |     Context cx = Context.enter();
89 |     cx.setErrorReporter(errorReporter);
90 |     cx.setLanguageVersion(Context.VERSION_1_5);
91 |     CompilerEnvirons compilerEnv = new CompilerEnvirons();
92 |     compilerEnv.initFromContext(cx);
93 |     compilerEnv.setRecordingComments(true);
94 |     compilerEnv.setRecordingLocalJsDocComments(true);
95 |     compilerEnv.setWarnTrailingComma(true);

99 |     }
100 | 
101 |     Parser p = new Parser(compilerEnv, errorReporter);
102 |     AstRoot astRoot = null;
103 |     try {
104 |       astRoot = p.parse(sourceString, sourceName, 1);
105 |     } catch (EvaluatorException e) {
106 |       logger.info("Error parsing " + sourceName + ": " + e.getMessage());
107 |     } finally {
108 |       Context.exit();
109 |     }
110 |     Node root = null;
111 |     if (astRoot != null) {
112 |       Config config = new Config(
113 |           typeRegistry, annotationNames, isIdeMode);
114 |       root = IRFactory.transformTree(
115 |           astRoot, sourceString, config, errorReporter);
116 |       root.setIsSyntheticBlock(true);
117 |     }
118 |     return root;

===== M0069 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Compiler.parseInputs()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Compiler.java
Original range: 944-1018
Documentation:
/**
   * Parses the externs and main inputs.
   *
   * @return A synthetic root node whose two children are the externs root
   *     and the main root
   */
Method code:
944 |   Node parseInputs() {
945 |     boolean devMode = options_.devMode != DevMode.OFF;
946 | 
947 |     // If old roots exist (we are parsing a second time), detach each of the
948 |     // individual file parse trees.
949 |     if (externsRoot != null) {
950 |       externsRoot.detachChildren();
951 |     }
952 |     if (jsRoot != null) {
953 |       jsRoot.detachChildren();
954 |     }
955 | 
956 |     // Parse main js sources.
957 |     jsRoot = new Node(Token.BLOCK);
958 |     jsRoot.setIsSyntheticBlock(true);
959 | 
960 |     if (options_.tracer.isOn()) {
961 |       tracker = new PerformanceTracker(jsRoot,
962 |           options_.tracer == TracerMode.ALL);
963 |       addChangeHandler(tracker.getCodeChangeHandler());
964 |     }
965 | 
966 |     Tracer tracer = newTracer("parseInputs");
967 | 
968 |     try {
969 |       // Parse externs sources.
970 |       externsRoot = new Node(Token.BLOCK);
971 |       externsRoot.setIsSyntheticBlock(true);
972 |       for (CompilerInput input : externs_) {
973 |         Node n = input.getAstRoot(this);
974 |         if (hasErrors()) {
975 |           return null;
976 |         }
977 |         externsRoot.addChildToBack(n);
978 |       }
979 | 
980 |       for (CompilerInput input : inputs_) {
981 |         Node n = input.getAstRoot(this);
982 |         if (hasErrors()) {
983 |           return null;
984 |         }
985 | 
986 |         // Inputs can have a null AST during initial parse.
987 |         if (n == null) {
988 |           continue;
989 |         }
990 | 
991 |         if (devMode) {
992 |           runSanityCheck();
993 |           if (hasErrors()) {
994 |             return null;
995 |           }
996 |         }
997 | 
998 |         if (options_.sourceMapOutputPath != null ||
999 |             options_.nameReferenceReportPath != null) {
1000 | 
1001 |           // Annotate the nodes in the tree with information from the
1002 |           // input file. This information is used to construct the SourceMap.
1003 |           SourceInformationAnnotator sia =
1004 |               new SourceInformationAnnotator(input.getName());
1005 |           NodeTraversal.traverse(this, n, sia);
1006 |         }
1007 | 
1008 |         jsRoot.addChildToBack(n);
1009 |       }
1010 | 
1011 |       externAndJsRoot = new Node(Token.BLOCK, externsRoot, jsRoot);
1012 |       externAndJsRoot.setIsSyntheticBlock(true);
1013 | 
1014 |       return externAndJsRoot;
1015 |     } finally {
1016 |       stopTracer(tracer, "parseInputs");
1017 |     }
1018 |   }

===== M0070 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.FoldConstants.tryFoldStringIndexOf(NodeTraversal,Node,Node,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/FoldConstants.java
Original range: 1415-1455
Documentation:
/**
   * Try to evaluate String.indexOf/lastIndexOf:
   *     "abcdef".indexOf("bc") -> 1
   *     "abcdefbc".indexOf("bc", 3) -> 6
   */
Method code:
1415 |   void tryFoldStringIndexOf(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node left, Node right,
1416 |                             Node parent) {
1417 |     if (!NodeUtil.isGetProp(left) || !NodeUtil.isImmutableValue(right)) {
1418 |       return;
1419 |     }
1420 | 
1421 |     Node lstringNode = left.getFirstChild();
1422 |     Node functionName = lstringNode.getNext();
1423 | 
1424 |     if ((lstringNode.getType() != Token.STRING) ||
1425 |         (!functionName.getString().equals("indexOf") &&
1426 |         !functionName.getString().equals("lastIndexOf"))) {
1427 |       return;
1428 |     }
1429 | 
1430 |     String lstring = NodeUtil.getStringValue(lstringNode);
1431 |     boolean isIndexOf = functionName.getString().equals("indexOf");
1432 |     Node firstArg = right;
1433 |     Node secondArg = right.getNext();
1434 |     String searchValue = NodeUtil.getStringValue(firstArg);
1435 |     // searchValue must be a valid string.
1436 |     if (searchValue == null) {
1437 |       return;
1438 |     }
1439 |     int fromIndex = isIndexOf ? 0 : lstring.length();
1440 |     if (secondArg != null) {
1441 |       // Third-argument and non-numeric second arg are problematic. Discard.
1442 |       if ((secondArg.getNext() != null) ||
1443 |           (secondArg.getType() != Token.NUMBER)) {
1444 |         return;
1445 |       } else {
1446 |         fromIndex = (int) secondArg.getDouble();
1447 |       }
1448 |     }
1449 |     int indexVal = isIndexOf ? lstring.indexOf(searchValue, fromIndex)
1450 |                              : lstring.lastIndexOf(searchValue, fromIndex);
1451 |     Node newNode = Node.newNumber(indexVal);
1452 |     parent.replaceChild(n, newNode);
1453 | 
1454 |     t.getCompiler().reportCodeChange();
1455 |   }

===== M0080 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CodeGenerator.addList(Node,int[])
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CodeGenerator.java
Original range: 743-762
Documentation:
/**
   * This function adds a comma-separated list as is specified by an ARRAYLIT
   * node with the associated skipIndexes array.  This is a space optimization
   * since we avoid creating a whole Node object for each empty array literal
   * slot.
   * @param firstInList The first in the node list (chained through the next
   * property).
   * @param skipIndexes If not null, then the array of skipped entries in the
   * array.
   */
Method code:
743 |   void addList(Node firstInList, int[] skipIndexes) {
744 |     int nextSlot = 0;
745 |     int nextSkipSlot = 0;
746 |     for (Node n = firstInList; n != null; n = n.getNext()) {
747 |       while (skipIndexes != null && nextSkipSlot < skipIndexes.length) {
748 |         if (nextSlot == skipIndexes[nextSkipSlot]) {
749 |           cc.listSeparator();
750 |           nextSlot++;
751 |           nextSkipSlot++;
752 |         } else {
753 |           break;
754 |         }
755 |       }
756 |       if (n != firstInList) {
757 |         cc.listSeparator();
758 |       }
759 |       addExpr(n, 1);
760 |       nextSlot++;
761 |     }
762 |   }

===== M0094 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.Node.checkTreeEqualsImpl(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/Node.java
Original range: 1350-1375
Documentation:
/**
     * Compare this node to node2 recursively and return the first pair
     * of nodes that differs doing a preorder depth-first traversal.
     * Package private for testing. Returns null if the nodes are equivalent.
     */
Method code:
1350 |     NodeMismatch checkTreeEqualsImpl(Node node2) {
1351 |         boolean eq = false;
1352 | 
1353 |         if (type == node2.getType() &&
1354 |             getChildCount() == node2.getChildCount() &&
1355 |             getClass() == node2.getClass()) {
1356 | 
1357 |             eq = this.isEquivalentTo(node2);
1358 |         }
1359 | 
1360 |         if (!eq) {
1361 |             return new NodeMismatch(this, node2);
1362 |         }
1363 | 
1364 |         NodeMismatch res = null;
1365 |         Node n, n2;
1366 |         for (n = first, n2 = node2.first;
1367 |              res == null && n != null;
1368 |              n = n.next, n2 = n2.next) {
1369 |             res = n.checkTreeEqualsImpl(n2);
1370 |             if (res != null) {
1371 |               return res;
1372 |             }
1373 |         }
1374 |         return res;
1375 |     }

===== M0102 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.IRFactory.TransformDispatcher.parseDirectives(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/IRFactory.java
Original range: 382-397
Documentation:
/**
     * Parse the directives, encode them in the AST, and remove their nodes.
     *
     * For information on ES5 directives, see section 14.1 of
     * Ecma-262, Edition 5.
     *
     * It would be nice if Rhino would eventually take care of this for
     * us, but right now their directive-processing is a one-off.
     */
Method code:
382 |     private void parseDirectives(Node node) {
383 |       // Remove all the directives, and encode them in the AST.
384 |       Set<String> directives = null;
385 |       while (isDirective(node.getFirstChild())) {
386 |         String directive = node.removeFirstChild().getFirstChild().getString();
387 |         if (directives == null) {
388 |           directives = Sets.newHashSet(directive);
389 |         } else {
390 |           directives.add(directive);
391 |         }
392 |       }
393 | 
394 |       if (directives != null) {
395 |         node.setDirectives(directives);
396 |       }
397 |     }

===== M0103 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CodeConsumer.addNumber(double)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CodeConsumer.java
Original range: 262-288
Documentation:

Method code:
262 |   void addNumber(double x) {
263 |     // This is not pretty printing. This is to prevent misparsing of x- -4 as
264 |     // x--4 (which is a syntax error).
265 |     char prev = getLastChar();
266 |     if (x < 0 && prev == '-') {
267 |       add(" ");
268 |     }
269 | 
270 |     if ((long) x == x) {
271 |       long value = (long) x;
272 |       long mantissa = value;
273 |       int exp = 0;
274 |       if (x >= 100) {
275 |         while (mantissa / 10 * Math.pow(10, exp + 1) == value) {
276 |           mantissa /= 10;
277 |           exp++;
278 |         }
279 |       }
280 |       if (exp > 2) {
281 |         add(Long.toString(mantissa) + "E" + Integer.toString(exp));
282 |       } else {
283 |         add(Long.toString(value));
284 |       }
285 |     } else {
286 |       add(String.valueOf(x));
287 |     }
288 |   }

   * Add blocks to IF, WHILE, DO, etc.

Method code:
129 |   private void normalizeBlocks(Node n) {
130 |     if (NodeUtil.isControlStructure(n)
131 |         && n.getType() != Token.LABEL
132 |         && n.getType() != Token.SWITCH) {
133 |       for (Node c = n.getFirstChild(); c != null; c = c.getNext()) {

140 |           } else {

145 |         }
146 |       }

===== M0127 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.PrototypeObjectType.isSubtype(JSType)

Original range: 360-404
Documentation:

Method code:
360 |   @Override
361 |   public boolean isSubtype(JSType that) {
362 |     if (JSType.isSubtype(this, that)) {
363 |       return true;
364 |     }
365 | 
366 |     // Union types
367 |     if (that instanceof UnionType) {
368 |       // The static {@code JSType.isSubtype} check already decomposed
369 |       // union types, so we don't need to check those again.

375 |       return RecordType.isSubtype(this, (RecordType) that);
376 |     }
377 | 
378 |     // Interfaces
379 |     // Find all the interfaces implemented by this class and compare each one
380 |     // to the interface instance.
381 |     ObjectType thatObj = that.toObjectType();
382 |     ObjectType thatCtor = thatObj == null ? null : thatObj.getConstructor();
383 |     if (thatCtor != null && thatCtor.isInterface()) {
384 |       Iterable<ObjectType> thisInterfaces = getCtorImplementedInterfaces();
385 |       for (ObjectType thisInterface : thisInterfaces) {
386 |         if (thisInterface.isSubtype(that)) {
387 |           return true;
388 |         }
389 |       }
390 |     }
391 | 
392 |     // other prototype based objects
393 |     if (that != null) {
394 |       if (isUnknownType() || implicitPrototypeChainIsUnknown()) {
395 |         // If unsure, say 'yes', to avoid spurious warnings.
396 |         // TODO(user): resolve the prototype chain completely in all cases,
397 |         // to avoid guessing.
398 |         return true;
399 |       }
400 |       return this.isImplicitPrototype(thatObj);
401 |     }
402 | 
403 |     return false;
404 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CodeGenerator.add(Node,Context)

Method code:
86 |   void add(Node n, Context context) {
87 |     if (!cc.continueProcessing()) {
88 |       return;
89 |     }
90 | 
91 |     int type = n.getType();
92 |     String opstr = NodeUtil.opToStr(type);
93 |     int childCount = n.getChildCount();
94 |     Node first = n.getFirstChild();
95 |     Node last = n.getLastChild();
96 | 
97 |     // Handle all binary operators
98 |     if (opstr != null && first != last) {
99 |       Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 2);
100 |       int p = NodeUtil.precedence(type);
101 |       addLeftExpr(first, p, context);
102 |       cc.addOp(opstr, true);
103 | 
104 |       // For right-hand-side of operations, only pass context if it's
105 |       // the IN_FOR_INIT_CLAUSE one.
106 |       Context rhsContext = getContextForNoInOperator(context);
107 | 
108 |       // Handle associativity.
109 |       // e.g. if the parse tree is a * (b * c),
110 |       // we can simply generate a * b * c.
111 |       if (last.getType() == type &&
112 |           NodeUtil.isAssociative(type)) {
113 |         addExpr(last, p, rhsContext);
114 |       } else if (NodeUtil.isAssignmentOp(n) && NodeUtil.isAssignmentOp(last)) {
115 |         // Assignments are the only right-associative binary operators
116 |         addExpr(last, p, rhsContext);
117 |       } else {
118 |         addExpr(last, p + 1, rhsContext);
119 |       }
120 |       return;
121 |     }
122 | 
123 |     cc.startSourceMapping(n);
124 | 
125 |     switch (type) {
126 |       case Token.TRY: {
127 |         Preconditions.checkState(first.getNext().getType() == Token.BLOCK &&
128 |                 first.getNext().getChildCount() <= 1);
129 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount >= 2 && childCount <= 3);
130 | 
131 |         add("try");
132 |         add(first, Context.PRESERVE_BLOCK);
133 | 
134 |         // second child contains the catch block, or nothing if there
135 |         // isn't a catch block
136 |         Node catchblock = first.getNext().getFirstChild();
137 |         if (catchblock != null) {
138 |           add(catchblock);
139 |         }
140 | 
141 |         if (childCount == 3) {
142 |           add("finally");
143 |           add(last, Context.PRESERVE_BLOCK);
144 |         }
145 |         break;
146 |       }
147 | 
148 |       case Token.CATCH:
149 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 3);
150 |         if (first.getNext().getType() != Token.EMPTY) {
151 |           throw new Error("Catch conditions not suppored because I think" +
152 |                           " that it may be a netscape only feature.");
153 |         }
154 | 
155 |         add("catch(");
156 |         add(first);
157 |         add(")");
158 |         add(last, Context.PRESERVE_BLOCK);
159 |         break;
160 | 
161 |       case Token.THROW:
162 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
163 |         add("throw");
164 |         add(first);
165 | 
166 |         // Must have a ';' after a throw statement, otherwise safari can't
167 |         // parse this.
168 |         cc.endStatement(true);
169 |         break;
170 | 
171 |       case Token.RETURN:
172 |         add("return");
173 |         if (childCount == 1) {
174 |           add(first);
175 |         } else {
176 |           Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 0);
177 |         }
178 |         cc.endStatement();
179 |         break;
180 | 
181 |       case Token.VAR:
182 |         if (first != null) {
183 |           add("var ");
184 |           addList(first, false, getContextForNoInOperator(context));
185 |         }
186 |         break;
187 | 
188 |       case Token.NAME:
189 |         if (first == null || first.getType() == Token.EMPTY) {
190 |           addIdentifier(n.getString());
191 |         } else {
192 |           Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
193 |           addIdentifier(n.getString());
194 |           cc.addOp("=", true);
195 |           if (first.getType() == Token.COMMA) {
196 |             addExpr(first, NodeUtil.precedence(Token.ASSIGN));
197 |           } else {
198 |             // Add expression, consider nearby code at lowest level of
199 |             // precedence.
200 |             addExpr(first, 0, getContextForNoInOperator(context));
201 |           }
202 |         }
203 | 
204 |         break;
205 | 
206 |       case Token.ARRAYLIT:
207 |         add("[");
208 |         addList(first, (int[]) n.getProp(Node.SKIP_INDEXES_PROP));
209 |         add("]");
210 |         break;
211 | 
212 |       case Token.LP:
213 |         add("(");
214 |         addList(first);
215 |         add(")");
216 |         break;
217 | 
218 |       case Token.COMMA:
219 |         addList(first, false, context);
220 |         break;
221 | 
222 |       case Token.NUMBER:
223 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 0);
224 |         cc.addNumber(n.getDouble());
225 |         break;
226 | 
227 |       case Token.TYPEOF:
228 |       case Token.VOID:
229 |       case Token.NOT:
230 |       case Token.BITNOT:
231 |       case Token.POS:
232 |       case Token.NEG: {
233 |         // All of these unary operators are right-associative
234 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
235 |         cc.addOp(NodeUtil.opToStrNoFail(type), false);
236 |         addExpr(first, NodeUtil.precedence(type));
237 |         break;
238 |       }
239 | 
240 |       case Token.HOOK: {
241 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 3);
242 |         int p = NodeUtil.precedence(type);
243 |         addLeftExpr(first, p + 1, context);
244 |         cc.addOp("?", true);
245 |         addExpr(first.getNext(), p);
246 |         cc.addOp(":", true);
247 |         addExpr(last, p);
248 |         break;
249 |       }
250 | 
251 |       case Token.REGEXP:
252 |         if (first.getType() != Token.STRING ||
253 |             last.getType() != Token.STRING) {
254 |           throw new Error("Expected children to be strings");
255 |         }
256 | 
257 |         String regexp = regexpEscape(first.getString(), outputCharsetEncoder);
258 | 
259 |         // I only use one .add because whitespace matters
260 |         if (childCount == 2) {
261 |           add(regexp + last.getString());
262 |         } else {
263 |           Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
264 |           add(regexp);
265 |         }
266 |         break;
267 | 
268 |       case Token.GET_REF:
269 |         add(first);
270 |         break;
271 | 
272 |       case Token.REF_SPECIAL:
273 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
274 |         add(first);
275 |         add(".");
276 |         add((String) n.getProp(Node.NAME_PROP));
277 |         break;
278 | 
279 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
280 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 3);
281 |         boolean funcNeedsParens = (context == Context.START_OF_EXPR);
282 |         if (funcNeedsParens) {
283 |           add("(");
284 |         }
285 | 
286 |         add("function");
287 |         add(first);
288 | 
289 |         add(first.getNext());
290 |         add(last, Context.PRESERVE_BLOCK);
291 |         cc.endFunction(context == Context.STATEMENT);
292 | 
293 |         if (funcNeedsParens) {
294 |           add(")");
295 |         }
296 |         break;
297 | 
298 |       case Token.SCRIPT:
299 |       case Token.BLOCK: {
300 |         boolean stripBlock = n.isSyntheticBlock() ||
301 |             ((context != Context.PRESERVE_BLOCK) && (n.getChildCount() < 2));
302 |         if (!stripBlock) {
303 |           cc.beginBlock();
304 |         }
305 |         for (Node c = first; c != null; c = c.getNext()) {
306 |           add(c, Context.STATEMENT);
307 | 
308 |           // VAR doesn't include ';' since it gets used in expressions
309 |           if (c.getType() == Token.VAR) {
310 |             cc.endStatement();
311 |           }
312 | 
313 |           if (c.getType() == Token.FUNCTION) {
314 |             cc.maybeLineBreak();
315 |           }
316 | 
317 |           // Prefer to break lines in between top-level statements
318 |           // because top level statements are more homogeneous.
319 |           if (type == Token.SCRIPT) {
320 |             cc.notePreferredLineBreak();
321 |           }
322 |         }
323 |         if (!stripBlock) {
324 |           cc.endBlock(context == Context.STATEMENT);
325 |         }
326 |         break;
327 |       }
328 | 
329 |       case Token.FOR:
330 |         if (childCount == 4) {
331 |           add("for(");
332 |           if (first.getType() == Token.VAR) {
333 |             add(first, Context.IN_FOR_INIT_CLAUSE);
334 |           } else {
335 |             addExpr(first, 0, Context.IN_FOR_INIT_CLAUSE);
336 |           }
337 |           add(";");
338 |           add(first.getNext());
339 |           add(";");
340 |           add(first.getNext().getNext());
341 |           add(")");
342 |           addNonEmptyExpression(
343 |               last, getContextForNonEmptyExpression(context), false);
344 |         } else {
345 |           Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 3);
346 |           add("for(");
347 |           add(first);
348 |           add("in");
349 |           add(first.getNext());
350 |           add(")");
351 |           addNonEmptyExpression(
352 |               last, getContextForNonEmptyExpression(context), false);
353 |         }
354 |         break;
355 | 
356 |       case Token.DO:
357 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 2);
358 |         add("do");
359 |         addNonEmptyExpression(first, Context.OTHER, false);
360 |         add("while(");
361 |         add(last);
362 |         add(")");
363 |         cc.endStatement();
364 |         break;
365 | 
366 |       case Token.WHILE:
367 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 2);
368 |         add("while(");
369 |         add(first);
370 |         add(")");
371 |         addNonEmptyExpression(
372 |             last, getContextForNonEmptyExpression(context), false);
373 |         break;
374 | 
375 |       case Token.EMPTY:
376 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 0);
377 |         break;
378 | 
379 |       case Token.GETPROP: {
380 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 2);
381 |         Preconditions.checkState(last.getType() == Token.STRING);
382 |         boolean needsParens = (first.getType() == Token.NUMBER);
383 |         if (needsParens) {
384 |           add("(");
385 |         }
386 |         addLeftExpr(first, NodeUtil.precedence(type), context);
387 |         if (needsParens) {
388 |           add(")");
389 |         }
390 |         add(".");
391 |         addIdentifier(last.getString());
392 |         break;
393 |       }
394 | 
395 |       case Token.GETELEM:
396 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 2);
397 |         addLeftExpr(first, NodeUtil.precedence(type), context);
398 |         add("[");
399 |         add(first.getNext());
400 |         add("]");
401 |         break;
402 | 
403 |       case Token.WITH:
404 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 2);
405 |         add("with(");
406 |         add(first);
407 |         add(")");
408 |         addNonEmptyExpression(
409 |             last, getContextForNonEmptyExpression(context), false);
410 |         break;
411 | 
412 |       case Token.INC:
413 |       case Token.DEC: {
414 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
415 |         String o = type == Token.INC ? "++" : "--";
416 |         int postProp = n.getIntProp(Node.INCRDECR_PROP, 0);
417 |         // A non-zero post-prop value indicates a post inc/dec, default of zero
418 |         // is a pre-inc/dec.
419 |         if (postProp != 0) {
420 |           addLeftExpr(first, NodeUtil.precedence(type), context);
421 |           cc.addOp(o, false);
422 |         } else {
423 |           cc.addOp(o, false);
424 |           add(first);
425 |         }
426 |         break;
427 |       }
428 | 
429 |       case Token.CALL:
430 |         // If the left hand side of the call is a direct reference to eval,
431 |         // then it must have a DIRECT_EVAL annotation. If it does not, then
432 |         // that means it was originally an indirect call to eval, and that
433 |         // indirectness must be preserved.
434 |         if (first.getType() == Token.NAME &&
435 |             "eval".equals(first.getString()) &&
436 |             !first.getBooleanProp(Node.DIRECT_EVAL)) {
437 |           add("(0,eval)");
438 |         } else {
439 |           addLeftExpr(first, NodeUtil.precedence(type), context);
440 |         }
441 |         add("(");
442 |         addList(first.getNext());
443 |         add(")");
444 |         break;
445 | 
446 |       case Token.IF:
447 |         boolean hasElse = childCount == 3;
448 |         boolean ambiguousElseClause =
449 |             context == Context.BEFORE_DANGLING_ELSE && !hasElse;
450 |         if (ambiguousElseClause) {
451 |           cc.beginBlock();
452 |         }
453 | 
454 |         add("if(");
455 |         add(first);
456 |         add(")");
457 | 
458 |         if (hasElse) {
459 |           addNonEmptyExpression(
460 |               first.getNext(), Context.BEFORE_DANGLING_ELSE, false);
461 |           add("else");
462 |           addNonEmptyExpression(
463 |               last, getContextForNonEmptyExpression(context), false);
464 |         } else {
465 |           addNonEmptyExpression(first.getNext(), Context.OTHER, false);
466 |           Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 2);
467 |         }
468 | 
469 |         if (ambiguousElseClause) {
470 |           cc.endBlock();
471 |         }
472 |         break;
473 | 
474 |       case Token.NULL:
475 |       case Token.THIS:
476 |       case Token.FALSE:
477 |       case Token.TRUE:
478 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 0);
479 |         add(Node.tokenToName(type));
480 |         break;
481 | 
482 |       case Token.CONTINUE:
483 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount <= 1);
484 |         add("continue");
485 |         if (childCount == 1) {
486 |           add(" ");
487 |           add(first);
488 |         }
489 |         cc.endStatement();
490 |         break;
491 | 
492 |       case Token.DEBUGGER:
493 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 0);
494 |         add("debugger");
495 |         cc.endStatement();
496 |         break;
497 | 
498 |       case Token.BREAK:
499 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount <= 1);
500 |         add("break");
501 |         if (childCount == 1) {
502 |           add(" ");
503 |           add(first);
504 |         }
505 |         cc.endStatement();
506 |         break;
507 | 
508 |       case Token.EXPR_VOID:
509 |       case Token.EXPR_RESULT:
510 |         if (type == Token.EXPR_VOID && validation) {
511 |           throw new Error("Unexpected EXPR_VOID. Should be EXPR_RESULT.");
512 |         }
513 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
514 |         add(first, Context.START_OF_EXPR);
515 |         cc.endStatement();
516 |         break;
517 | 
518 |       case Token.NEW:
519 |         add("new ");
520 |         int precedence = NodeUtil.precedence(type);
521 | 
522 |         // If the first child contains a CALL, then claim higher precedence
523 |         // to force parens. Otherwise, when parsed, NEW will bind to the
524 |         // first viable parens
525 |         if (NodeUtil.containsCall(first)) {
526 |           precedence = NodeUtil.precedence(first.getType()) + 1;
527 |         }
528 |         addExpr(first, precedence);
529 | 
530 |         // '()' is optional when no arguments are present
531 |         Node next = first.getNext();
532 |         if (next != null) {
533 |           add("(");
534 |           addList(next);
535 |           add(")");
536 |         }
537 |         break;
538 | 
539 |       case Token.STRING:
540 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 0);
541 |         add(jsString(n.getString(), outputCharsetEncoder));
542 |         break;
543 | 
544 |       case Token.DELPROP:
545 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
546 |         add("delete ");
547 |         add(first);
548 |         break;
549 | 
550 |       case Token.OBJECTLIT: {
551 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount % 2 == 0);
552 |         boolean needsParens = (context == Context.START_OF_EXPR);
553 |         if (needsParens) {
554 |           add("(");
555 |         }
556 |         add("{");
557 |         for (Node c = first; c != null; c = c.getNext().getNext()) {
558 |           if (c != first) {
559 |             cc.listSeparator();
560 |           }
561 | 
562 |           // Object literal property names don't have to be quoted if they are
563 |           // not JavaScript keywords
564 |           if (c.getType() == Token.STRING &&
565 |               !TokenStream.isKeyword(c.getString()) &&
566 |               TokenStream.isJSIdentifier(c.getString()) &&
567 |               // do not encode literally any non-literal characters that were
568 |               // unicode escaped.
569 |               NodeUtil.isLatin(c.getString())) {
570 |             add(c.getString());
571 |           } else {
572 |             addExpr(c, 1);
573 |           }
574 |           add(":");
575 |           addExpr(c.getNext(), 1);
576 |         }
577 |         add("}");
578 |         if (needsParens) {
579 |           add(")");
580 |         }
581 |         break;
582 |       }
583 | 
584 |       case Token.SWITCH:
585 |         add("switch(");
586 |         add(first);
587 |         add(")");
588 |         cc.beginBlock();
589 |         addAllSiblings(first.getNext());
590 |         cc.endBlock(context == Context.STATEMENT);
591 |         break;
592 | 
593 |       case Token.CASE:
594 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 2);
595 |         add("case ");
596 |         add(first);
597 |         addCaseBody(last);
598 |         break;
599 | 
600 |       case Token.DEFAULT:
601 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
602 |         add("default");
603 |         addCaseBody(first);
604 |         break;
605 | 
606 |       case Token.LABEL:
607 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 2);
608 |         add(first);
609 |         add(":");
610 |         addNonEmptyExpression(
611 |             last, getContextForNonEmptyExpression(context), true);
612 |         break;
613 | 
614 |       // This node is auto generated in anonymous functions and should just get
615 |       // ignored for our purposes.
616 |       case Token.SETNAME:
617 |         break;
618 | 
619 |       default:
620 |         throw new Error("Unknown type " + type + "\n" + n.toStringTree());
621 |     }
622 | 
623 |     cc.endSourceMapping(n);
624 |   }

===== M0140 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.FunctionType.getPrototype()

Original range: 102-124
Documentation:
/**
   * Covert EXPR_VOID to EXPR_RESULT to simplify the rest of the code.
   */
Method code:
102 |   private void normalizeNodeTypes(Node n) {
103 |     if (n.getType() == Token.EXPR_VOID) {
104 |       n.setType(Token.EXPR_RESULT);
105 |       reportChange();
106 |     }
107 | 
108 |     // Remove unused properties to minimize differences between ASTs
109 |     // produced by the two parsers.
110 |     if (n.getType() == Token.FUNCTION) {
111 |       Preconditions.checkState(n.getProp(Node.FUNCTION_PROP) == null);
112 |     }
113 | 
114 |     normalizeBlocks(n);
115 | 
116 |     for (Node child = n.getFirstChild();
117 |          child != null; child = child.getNext()) {
118 |       // This pass is run during the CompilerTestCase validation, so this
119 |       // parent pointer check serves as a more general check.
120 |       Preconditions.checkState(child.getParent() == n);
121 | 
122 |       normalizeNodeTypes(child);
123 |     }
124 |   }

// Gets all the property types, in sorted order.
Method code:
802 |     private int[] getSortedPropTypes() {
803 |         int count = 0;
804 |         for (PropListItem x = propListHead; x != null; x = x.next) {
805 |             count++;
806 |         }
807 | 
808 |         int[] keys = new int[count];
809 |         for (PropListItem x = propListHead; x != null; x = x.next) {
810 |             count--;
811 |             keys[count] = x.type;
812 |         }
813 | 
814 |         Arrays.sort(keys);
815 |         return keys;
816 |     }

===== M0150 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.FoldConstants.tryFoldStringJoin(NodeTraversal,Node,Node,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/FoldConstants.java
Original range: 1461-1549
Documentation:
/**
   * Try to fold an array join: ['a', 'b', 'c'].join('') -> 'abc';
   */
Method code:
1461 |   void tryFoldStringJoin(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node left, Node right,
1462 |                          Node parent) {
1463 |     if (!NodeUtil.isGetProp(left) || !NodeUtil.isImmutableValue(right)) {
1464 |       return;
1465 |     }
1466 | 
1467 |     Node arrayNode = left.getFirstChild();
1468 |     Node functionName = arrayNode.getNext();
1469 | 
1470 |     if ((arrayNode.getType() != Token.ARRAYLIT) ||
1471 |         !functionName.getString().equals("join")) {
1472 |       return;
1473 |     }
1474 | 
1475 |     String joinString = NodeUtil.getStringValue(right);
1476 |     List<Node> arrayFoldedChildren = Lists.newLinkedList();
1477 |     StringBuilder sb = new StringBuilder();
1478 |     int foldedSize = 0;
1479 |     Node elem = arrayNode.getFirstChild();
1480 |     // Merges adjacent String nodes.
1481 |     while (elem != null) {
1482 |       if (NodeUtil.isImmutableValue(elem)) {
1483 |         if (sb.length() > 0) {
1484 |           sb.append(joinString);
1485 |         }
1486 |         sb.append(NodeUtil.getStringValue(elem));
1487 |       } else {
1488 |         if (sb.length() > 0) {
1489 |           // + 2 for the quotes.
1490 |           foldedSize += sb.length() + 2;
1491 |           arrayFoldedChildren.add(Node.newString(sb.toString()));
1492 |           sb = new StringBuilder();
1493 |         }
1494 |         foldedSize += InlineCostEstimator.getCost(elem);
1495 |         arrayFoldedChildren.add(elem);
1496 |       }
1497 |       elem = elem.getNext();
1498 |     }
1499 | 
1500 |     if (sb.length() > 0) {
1501 |       // + 2 for the quotes.
1502 |       foldedSize += sb.length() + 2;
1503 |       arrayFoldedChildren.add(Node.newString(sb.toString()));
1504 |     }
1505 |     // one for each comma.
1506 |     foldedSize += arrayFoldedChildren.size() - 1;
1507 | 
1508 |     int originalSize = InlineCostEstimator.getCost(n);
1509 |     switch (arrayFoldedChildren.size()) {
1510 |       case 0:
1511 |         Node emptyStringNode = Node.newString("");
1512 |         parent.replaceChild(n, emptyStringNode);
1513 |         break;
1514 | 
1515 |       case 1:
1516 |         Node foldedStringNode = arrayFoldedChildren.remove(0);
1517 |         if (foldedSize > originalSize) {
1518 |           return;
1519 |         }
1520 |         arrayNode.detachChildren();
1521 |         if (foldedStringNode.getType() != Token.STRING) {
1522 |           // If the Node is not a string literal, ensure that
1523 |           // it is coerced to a string.
1524 |           Node replacement = new Node(Token.ADD,
1525 |               Node.newString(""), foldedStringNode);
1526 |           foldedStringNode = replacement;
1527 |         }
1528 |         parent.replaceChild(n, foldedStringNode);
1529 |         break;
1530 | 
1531 |       default:
1532 |         // No folding could actually be performed.
1533 |         if (arrayFoldedChildren.size() == arrayNode.getChildCount()) {
1534 |           return;
1535 |         }
1536 |         int kJoinOverhead = "[].join()".length();
1537 |         foldedSize += kJoinOverhead;
1538 |         foldedSize += InlineCostEstimator.getCost(right);
1539 |         if (foldedSize > originalSize) {
1540 |           return;
1541 |         }
1542 |         arrayNode.detachChildren();
1543 |         for (Node node : arrayFoldedChildren) {
1544 |           arrayNode.addChildToBack(node);
1545 |         }
1546 |         break;
1547 |     }
1548 |     t.getCompiler().reportCodeChange();
1549 |   }

/**
   * Sets the prototype.
   * @param prototype the prototype. If this value is {@code null} it will
   *        silently be discarded.
   */
Method code:
337 |   public boolean setPrototype(FunctionPrototypeType prototype) {
338 |     if (prototype == null) {
339 |       return false;

352 |       }
353 |     }

   * We treat this as the unknown type if any of its implicit prototype
   * properties is unknown.
   */
Method code:
403 |   @Override
404 |   public boolean isUnknownType() {
405 |     // If the object is unknown now, check the supertype again,
406 |     // because it might have been resolved since the last check.
407 |     if (unknown) {
408 |       ObjectType implicitProto = getImplicitPrototype();

412 |       } else {

414 |       }
415 |     }
416 |     return unknown;
417 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CodePrinter.CompactCodePrinter.maybeLineBreak()

Method code:
428 |     @Override
429 |     void maybeLineBreak() {
430 |       if (lineBreak) {
431 |         if (sawFunction) {
432 |           startNewLine();
433 |           sawFunction = false;
434 |         }
435 |       }
436 | 
437 |       // Since we are at a legal line break, can we upgrade the
438 |       // preferred break position?  We prefer to break after a
439 |       // semicolon rather than before it.
440 |       int len = code.length();
441 |       if (preferredBreakPosition == len - 1) {
442 |         char ch = code.charAt(len - 1);
443 |         if (ch == ';') {
444 |           preferredBreakPosition = len;
445 |         }
446 |       }
447 |       maybeCutLine();
448 |     }

===== M0170 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.UnionTypeBuilder.addAlternate(JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/UnionTypeBuilder.java
Original range: 85-129
Documentation:
/**
   * Adds an alternate to the union type under construction. Returns this
   * for easy chaining.
   */
Method code:
85 |   UnionTypeBuilder addAlternate(JSType alternate) {
86 |     // build() returns the bottom type by default, so we can
87 |     // just bail out early here.
88 |     if (alternate.isNoType()) {
89 |       return this;
90 |     }
91 | 
92 |     isAllType = isAllType || alternate.isAllType();
93 | 
94 |     boolean isAlternateUnknown = alternate instanceof UnknownType;
95 |     isNativeUnknownType = isNativeUnknownType || isAlternateUnknown;
96 |     if (isAlternateUnknown) {
97 |       areAllUnknownsChecked = areAllUnknownsChecked &&
98 |           alternate.isCheckedUnknownType();
99 |     }
100 |     if (!isAllType && !isNativeUnknownType) {
101 |       if (alternate instanceof UnionType) {
102 |         UnionType union = (UnionType) alternate;
103 |         for (JSType unionAlt : union.getAlternates()) {
104 |           addAlternate(unionAlt);
105 |         }
106 |       } else {
107 |         if (!alternate.isUnknownType()) {
108 |           Iterator<JSType> it = alternates.iterator();
109 |           while (it.hasNext()) {
110 |             JSType current = it.next();
111 |             if (!current.isUnknownType()) {
112 |               if (alternate.isSubtype(current)) {
113 |                 // Alternate is unnecessary.
114 |                 return this;
115 |               } else if (current.isSubtype(alternate)) {
116 |                 // Alternate makes current obsolete
117 |                 it.remove();
118 |               }
119 |             }
120 |           }
121 |         }
122 |         alternates.add(alternate);
123 |         result = null; // invalidate the memoized result
124 |       }
125 |     } else {
126 |       result = null;
127 |     }
128 |     return this;
129 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.Node.toString(StringBuilder,boolean,boolean,boolean)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/Node.java
Original range: 888-1001
Documentation:

Method code:
888 |     private void toString(
889 |         StringBuilder sb,
890 |         boolean printSource,
891 |         boolean printAnnotations,
892 |         boolean printType)
893 |     {
894 |         if (Token.printTrees) {
895 |             sb.append(Token.name(type));
896 |             if (this instanceof StringNode) {
897 |                 sb.append(' ');
898 |                 sb.append(getString());
899 |             } else if (type == Token.FUNCTION) {
900 |                 sb.append(' ');
901 |                 sb.append(first.getString());
902 |             } else if (this instanceof ScriptOrFnNode) {
903 |                 ScriptOrFnNode sof = (ScriptOrFnNode)this;
904 |                 if (this instanceof FunctionNode) {
905 |                     FunctionNode fn = (FunctionNode)this;
906 |                     sb.append(' ');
907 |                     sb.append(fn.getFunctionName());
908 |                 }
909 |                 if (printSource) {
910 |                   sb.append(" [source name: ");
911 |                   sb.append(sof.getSourceName());
912 |                   sb.append("] [encoded source length: ");
913 |                   sb.append(sof.getEncodedSourceEnd()
914 |                             - sof.getEncodedSourceStart());
915 |                   sb.append("] [base line: ");
916 |                   sb.append(sof.getBaseLineno());
917 |                   sb.append("] [end line: ");
918 |                   sb.append(sof.getEndLineno());
919 |                   sb.append(']');
920 |                 }
921 |             } else if (type == Token.NUMBER) {
922 |                 sb.append(' ');
923 |                 sb.append(getDouble());
924 |             }
925 |             if (printSource) {
926 |               int lineno = getLineno();
927 |               if (lineno != -1) {
928 |                   sb.append(' ');
929 |                   sb.append(lineno);
930 |               }
931 |             }
932 | 
933 |             if (printAnnotations) {
934 |               int[] keys = getSortedPropTypes();
935 |               for (int i = 0; i < keys.length; i++) {
936 |                   int type = keys[i];
937 |                   PropListItem x = lookupProperty(type);
938 |                   sb.append(" [");
939 |                   sb.append(propToString(type));
940 |                   sb.append(": ");
941 |                   String value;
942 |                   switch (type) {
943 |                     case TARGETBLOCK_PROP : // can't add this as it recurses
944 |                       value = "target block property";
945 |                       break;
946 |                     case LOCAL_BLOCK_PROP :     // can't add this as it is dull
947 |                       value = "last local block";
948 |                       break;
949 |                     case ISNUMBER_PROP:
950 |                       switch (x.intValue) {
951 |                         case BOTH:
952 |                           value = "both";
953 |                           break;
954 |                         case RIGHT:
955 |                           value = "right";
956 |                           break;
957 |                         case LEFT:
958 |                           value = "left";
959 |                           break;
960 |                         default:
961 |                           throw Kit.codeBug();
962 |                       }
963 |                       break;
964 |                     case SPECIALCALL_PROP:
965 |                       switch (x.intValue) {
966 |                         case SPECIALCALL_EVAL:
967 |                           value = "eval";
968 |                           break;
969 |                         case SPECIALCALL_WITH:
970 |                           value = "with";
971 |                           break;
972 |                         default:
973 |                           // NON_SPECIALCALL should not be stored
974 |                           throw Kit.codeBug();
975 |                       }
976 |                       break;
977 |                     default :
978 |                       Object obj = x.objectValue;
979 |                       if (obj != null) {
980 |                           value = obj.toString();
981 |                       } else {
982 |                           value = String.valueOf(x.intValue);
983 |                       }
984 |                       break;
985 |                   }
986 |                   sb.append(value);
987 |                   sb.append(']');
988 |               }
989 |             }
990 | 
991 |             if (printType) {
992 |               if (jsType != null) {
993 |                   String jsTypeString = jsType.toString();
994 |                   if (jsTypeString != null) {
995 |                       sb.append(" : ");
996 |                       sb.append(jsTypeString);
997 |                   }
998 |               }
999 |             }
1000 |         }
1001 |     }

===== M0179 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.Node.mergeLineCharNo(int,int)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/Node.java
Original range: 1109-1117
Documentation:
/**
     * Merges the line number and character number in one integer. The Character
     * number takes the first 12 bits and the line number takes the rest. If
     * the character number is greater than <code>2<sup>12</sup>-1</code> it is
     * adjusted to <code>2<sup>12</sup>-1</code>.
     */
Method code:
1109 |     protected static int mergeLineCharNo(int lineno, int charno) {
1110 |       if (lineno < 0 || charno < 0) {
1111 |         return -1;
1112 |       } else if ((charno & ~COLUMN_MASK) != 0) {
1113 |         return lineno << COLUMN_BITS | COLUMN_MASK;
1114 |       } else {
1115 |         return lineno << COLUMN_BITS | (charno & COLUMN_MASK);
1116 |       }
1117 |     }

739 |     {
740 |         PropListItem x = propListHead;

/**
   * Checks that the prototype is an implicit prototype of this object. Since
   * each object has an implicit prototype, an implicit prototype's
   * implicit prototype is also this implicit prototype's.
   *
   * @param prototype any prototype based object
   *
   * @return {@code true} if {@code prototype} is {@code equal} to any
   *         object in this object's implicit prototype chain.
   */

383 |   final boolean isImplicitPrototype(ObjectType prototype) {

387 |       if (current.equals(prototype)) {
388 |         return true;
389 |       }
390 |     }
391 |     return false;
392 |   }

===== M0226 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.checkForStateChangeHelper(Node,boolean)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 352-478
Documentation:
/**
   * Returns true if some node in n's subtree changes application state.
   * If {@code checkForNewObjects} is true, we assume that newly created
   * mutable objects (like object literals) change state. Otherwise, we assume
   * that they have no side effects.
   */
Method code:
352 |   private static boolean checkForStateChangeHelper(
353 |       Node n, boolean checkForNewObjects) {
354 |     // Rather than id which ops may have side effects, id the ones
355 |     // that we know to be safe
356 |     switch (n.getType()) {
357 |       // other side-effect free statements and expressions
358 |       case Token.AND:
359 |       case Token.BLOCK:
360 |       case Token.EXPR_RESULT:
361 |       case Token.HOOK:
362 |       case Token.IF:
363 |       case Token.IN:
364 |       case Token.LP:
365 |       case Token.NUMBER:
366 |       case Token.OR:
367 |       case Token.THIS:
368 |       case Token.TRUE:
369 |       case Token.FALSE:
370 |       case Token.NULL:
371 |       case Token.STRING:
372 |       case Token.SWITCH:
373 |       case Token.TRY:
374 |       case Token.EMPTY:
375 |         break;
376 | 
377 |       // Throws are by definition side effects
378 |       case Token.THROW:
379 |         return true;
380 | 
381 |       case Token.OBJECTLIT:
382 |       case Token.ARRAYLIT:
383 |       case Token.REGEXP:
384 |         if (checkForNewObjects) {
385 |           return true;
386 |         }
387 |         break;
388 | 
389 |       case Token.VAR:    // empty var statement (no declaration)
390 |       case Token.NAME:   // variable by itself
391 |         if (n.getFirstChild() != null)
392 |           return true;
393 | 
394 |         break;
395 | 
396 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
397 |         // Anonymous functions don't have side-effects, but named ones
398 |         // change the namespace. Therefore, we check if the function has
399 |         // a name. Either way, we don't need to check the children, since
400 |         // they aren't executed at declaration time.
401 |         //
402 |         return !isFunctionAnonymous(n);
403 | 
404 |       case Token.NEW:
405 |         {
406 |           if (checkForNewObjects) {
407 |             return true;
408 |           }
409 | 
410 |           // calls to constructors that have no side effects have the
411 |           // no side effect property set.
412 |           if (n.isNoSideEffectsCall()) {
413 |             break;
414 |           }
415 | 
416 |           // certain constructors are certified side effect free
417 |           Node constructor = n.getFirstChild();
418 |           if (Token.NAME == constructor.getType()) {
419 |             String className = constructor.getString();
420 |             if (CONSTRUCTORS_WITHOUT_SIDE_EFFECTS.contains(className)) {
421 |               // loop below will see if the constructor parameters have
422 |               // side-effects
423 |               break;
424 |             }
425 |           } else {
426 |             // the constructor could also be an expression like
427 |             // new (useArray ? Object : Array)();
428 |           }
429 |         }
430 |         return true;
431 | 
432 |       case Token.CALL:
433 |         // calls to functions that have no side effects have the no
434 |         // side effect property set.
435 |         if (n.isNoSideEffectsCall()) {
436 |           // loop below will see if the function parameters have
437 |           // side-effects
438 |           break;
439 |         }
440 |         return true;
441 | 
442 |       default:
443 |         if (isSimpleOperatorType(n.getType()))
444 |           break;
445 | 
446 |         if (isAssignmentOp(n)) {
447 |           // Assignments will have side effects if
448 |           // a) The RHS has side effects, or
449 |           // b) The LHS has side effects, or
450 |           // c) A name on the LHS will exist beyond the life of this statement.
451 |           if (checkForStateChangeHelper(
452 |                   n.getFirstChild(), checkForNewObjects) ||
453 |               checkForStateChangeHelper(
454 |                   n.getLastChild(), checkForNewObjects)) {
455 |             return true;
456 |           }
457 | 
458 |           Node current = n.getFirstChild();
459 |           for (;
460 |                current.getType() == Token.GETPROP ||
461 |                current.getType() == Token.GETELEM;
462 |                current = current.getFirstChild()) { }
463 | 
464 |           return !(isLiteralValue(current) ||
465 |               current.getType() == Token.FUNCTION);
466 |         }
467 | 
468 |         return true;
469 |     }
470 | 
471 |     for (Node c = n.getFirstChild(); c != null; c = c.getNext()) {
472 |       if (checkForStateChangeHelper(c, checkForNewObjects)) {
473 |         return true;
474 |       }
475 |     }
476 | 
477 |     return false;
478 |   }

/**
   * Try removing unneeded block nodes and their useless children
   */
Method code:
478 |   void tryFoldBlock(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
479 |     // Remove any useless children
480 |     for (Node c = n.getFirstChild(); c != null; ) {
481 |       Node next = c.getNext();  // save c.next, since 'c' may be removed
482 |       if (!NodeUtil.mayHaveSideEffects(c)) {
483 |         n.removeChild(c);  // lazy kids
484 |         t.getCompiler().reportCodeChange();
485 |       }
486 |       c = next;
487 |     }
488 | 
489 |     if (n.isSyntheticBlock() || parent == null) {
490 |       return;
491 |     }
492 | 
493 |     // Try to remove the block.
494 |     if (NodeUtil.tryMergeBlock(n)) {
495 |       t.getCompiler().reportCodeChange();
496 |     }
497 |   }

/**
     * Detaches child from Node and replaces it with newChild.
     */
Method code:
672 |     public void replaceChild(Node child, Node newChild) {
673 |         Preconditions.checkArgument(newChild.next == null,
674 |             "The new child node has siblings.");
675 |         Preconditions.checkArgument(newChild.parent == null,
676 |             "The new child node already has a parent.");
677 | 
678 |         // Copy over important information.
679 |         newChild.copyInformationFrom(child);
680 | 
681 |         newChild.next = child.next;
682 |         newChild.parent = this;
683 |         if (child == first) {
684 |             first = newChild;
685 |         } else {
686 |             Node prev = getChildBefore(child);
687 |             prev.next = newChild;
688 |         }
689 |         if (child == last)
690 |             last = newChild;
691 |         child.next = null;
692 |         child.parent = null;
693 |     }

===== M0269 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.JSType.isSubtype(JSType,JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/JSType.java
Original range: 802-829
Documentation:
/**
   * A generic implementation meant to be used as a helper for common subtyping
   * cases.
   */
Method code:
802 |   static boolean isSubtype(JSType thisType, JSType thatType) {
803 |     // unknown
804 |     if (thatType.isUnknownType()) {
805 |       return true;
806 |     }
807 |     // equality
808 |     if (thisType.equals(thatType)) {
809 |       return true;
810 |     }
811 |     // all type
812 |     if (thatType.isAllType()) {
813 |       return true;
814 |     }
815 |     // unions
816 |     if (thatType instanceof UnionType) {
817 |       UnionType union = (UnionType)thatType;
818 |       for (JSType element : union.alternates) {
819 |         if (thisType.isSubtype(element)) {
820 |           return true;
821 |         }
822 |       }
823 |     }
824 |     // named types
825 |     if (thatType instanceof NamedType) {
826 |       return thisType.isSubtype(((NamedType)thatType).referencedType);
827 |     }
828 |     return false;
829 |   }

279 |           case IFEQ:            return "IFEQ";
280 |           case IFNE:            return "IFNE";

===== M0285 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.JsAst.parse(AbstractCompiler,String,String)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/JsAst.java
Original range: 85-108
Documentation:

Method code:
85 |   private void parse(AbstractCompiler compiler, String sourceName,
86 |       String sourceStr) {
87 |     try {
88 |       logger_.fine("Parsing: " + sourceName);
89 |       root = ParserRunner.parse(sourceName, sourceStr, compiler.isIdeMode(),
90 |           compiler.getTypeRegistry(),
91 |           compiler.getDefaultErrorReporter(),
92 |           logger_);
93 |     } catch (IOException e) {
94 |       compiler.report(JSError.make(AbstractCompiler.READ_ERROR, sourceName));
95 |     }
96 | 
97 |     if (root == null || compiler.hasHaltingErrors()) {
98 |       // There was a parse error or IOException, so use a dummy block.
99 |       root = new Node(Token.BLOCK);
100 |     } else {
101 |       compiler.normalizeNodeTypes(root);
102 |       compiler.annotateCodingConvention(root);
103 |     }
104 | 
105 |     // Set the source name so that the compiler passes can track
106 |     // the source file and module.
107 |     root.putProp(Node.SOURCENAME_PROP, sourceName);
108 |   }

===== M0287 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CodeGenerator.identifierEscape(String)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CodeGenerator.java
Original range: 889-909
Documentation:

Method code:
889 |   static String identifierEscape(String s) {
890 |     // First check if escaping is needed at all -- in most cases it isn't.
891 |     if (NodeUtil.isLatin(s)) {
892 |       return s;
893 |     }
894 | 
895 |     // Now going through the string to escape non-latin characters if needed.
896 |     StringBuilder sb = new StringBuilder();
897 |     for (int i = 0; i < s.length(); i++) {
898 |       char c = s.charAt(i);
899 |       // Identifiers should always go to Latin1/ ASCII characters because
900 |       // different browser's rules for valid identifier characters are
901 |       // crazy.
902 |       if (c > 0x1F && c < 0x7F) {
903 |         sb.append(c);
904 |       } else {
905 |         appendHexJavaScriptRepresentation(sb, c);
906 |       }
907 |     }
908 |     return sb.toString();
909 |   }

===== M0301 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeTraversal.traverseBranch(Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeTraversal.java
Original range: 441-478
Documentation:
/**
   * Traverses a branch.
   */
Method code:
441 |   @SuppressWarnings("fallthrough")
442 |   private void traverseBranch(Node n, Node parent) {
443 |     int type = n.getType();
444 |     if (type == Token.SCRIPT) {
445 |       sourceName = getSourceName(n);
446 |     }
447 | 
448 |     curNode = n;
449 |     if (!callback.shouldTraverse(this, n, parent)) return;
450 | 
451 |     switch (type) {
452 |       case Token.CATCH:
453 |         Preconditions.checkState(n.getChildCount() == 3);
454 |         Preconditions.checkState(n.getFirstChild().getType() == Token.NAME);
455 |         // the first child is the catch var and the third child
456 |         // is the code block
457 |         traverseBranch(n.getFirstChild(), n);
458 |         traverseBranch(n.getFirstChild().getNext().getNext(), n);
459 |         break;
460 | 
461 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
462 |         traverseFunction(n, parent);
463 |         break;
464 | 
465 |       default:
466 |         for (Node child = n.getFirstChild(); child != null; ) {
467 |           // child could be replaced, in which case our child node
468 |           // would no longer point to the true next
469 |           Node next = child.getNext();
470 |           traverseBranch(child, n);
471 |           child = next;
472 |         }
473 |         break;
474 |     }
475 | 
476 |     curNode = n;
477 |     callback.visit(this, n, parent);
478 |   }

===== M0306 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CodeGenerator.strEscape(String,char,String,String,String,CharsetEncoder)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CodeGenerator.java
Original range: 826-887
Documentation:
/** Helper to escape javascript string as well as regular expression */
Method code:
826 |   static String strEscape(String s, char quote,
827 |                           String doublequoteEscape,
828 |                           String singlequoteEscape,
829 |                           String backslashEscape,
830 |                           CharsetEncoder outputCharsetEncoder) {
831 |     StringBuilder sb = new StringBuilder();
832 |     sb.append(quote);
833 |     for (int i = 0; i < s.length(); i++) {
834 |       char c = s.charAt(i);
835 |       switch (c) {
836 |         case '\n': sb.append("\\n"); break;
837 |         case '\r': sb.append("\\r"); break;
838 |         case '\t': sb.append("\\t"); break;
839 |         case '\\': sb.append(backslashEscape); break;
840 |         case '\"': sb.append(doublequoteEscape); break;
841 |         case '\'': sb.append(singlequoteEscape); break;
842 |         case '>':                       // Break --> into --\> or ]]> into ]]\>
843 |           if (i >= 2 &&
844 |               ((s.charAt(i - 1) == '-' && s.charAt(i - 2) == '-') ||
845 |                (s.charAt(i - 1) == ']' && s.charAt(i - 2) == ']'))) {
846 |             sb.append("\\>");
847 |           } else {
848 |             sb.append(c);
849 |           }
850 |           break;
851 |         case '<':                       // Break </script into <\/script
852 |           final String END_SCRIPT = "/script";
853 |           if (s.regionMatches(true, i + 1, END_SCRIPT, 0,
854 |               END_SCRIPT.length())) {
855 |             sb.append("<\\");
856 |           } else {
857 |             sb.append(c);
858 |           }
859 |           break;
860 |         default:
861 |           // If we're given an outputCharsetEncoder, then check if the
862 |           //  character can be represented in this character set.
863 |           if (outputCharsetEncoder != null) {
864 |             if (outputCharsetEncoder.canEncode(c)) {
865 |               sb.append(c);
866 |             } else {
867 |               // Unicode-escape the character.
868 |               appendHexJavaScriptRepresentation(sb, c);
869 |             }
870 |           } else {
871 |             // No charsetEncoder provided - pass straight latin characters
872 |             // through, and escape the rest.  Doing the explicit character
873 |             // check is measurably faster than using the CharsetEncoder.
874 |             if (c > 0x1f && c <= 0x7f) {
875 |               sb.append(c);
876 |             } else {
877 |               // Other characters can be misinterpreted by some js parsers,
878 |               // or perhaps mangled by proxies along the way,
879 |               // so we play it safe and unicode escape them.
880 |               appendHexJavaScriptRepresentation(sb, c);
881 |             }
882 |           }
883 |       }
884 |     }
885 |     sb.append(quote);
886 |     return sb.toString();
887 |   }

===== M0322 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.isLiteralValue(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 205-223
Documentation:
/**
   * Returns true if this is a literal value. We define a literal value
   * as any node that evaluates to the same thing regardless of when or
   * where it is evaluated. So /xyz/ and [3, 5] are literals, but
   * function() { return a; } is not.
   */
Method code:
205 |   static boolean isLiteralValue(Node n) {
206 |     // TODO(nicksantos): Refine this function to catch more literals.
207 |     switch (n.getType()) {
208 |       case Token.ARRAYLIT:
209 |       case Token.OBJECTLIT:
210 |       case Token.REGEXP:
211 |         // Return true only if all children are const.
212 |         for (Node child = n.getFirstChild(); child != null;
213 |              child = child.getNext()) {
214 |           if (!isLiteralValue(child)) {
215 |             return false;
216 |           }
217 |         }
218 |         return true;
219 | 
220 |       default:
221 |         return isImmutableValue(n);
222 |     }
223 |   }
