Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.RhinoErrorReporter.replacePlaceHolders(String)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/RhinoErrorReporter.java
Original range: 62-65
Documentation:
/**
   * For each message such as "Not a good use of {0}", replace the place
   * holder {0} with a wild card that matches all possible strings.
   * Also put the any non-place-holder in quotes for regex matching later.
   */
Method code:
62 |   private String replacePlaceHolders(String s) {
63 |     s = Pattern.quote(s);
64 |     return s.replaceAll("\\{\\d+\\}", "\\\\E.*\\\\Q");
65 |   }

===== M0324 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.FoldConstants.tryMinimizeCondition(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/FoldConstants.java
Original range: 1861-1924
Documentation:
/**
   * Try to minimize conditions expressions, as there are additional
   * assumptions that can be made when it is known that the final result
   * is a boolean.
   *
   * The following transformations are done recursively:
   *   !(x||y) --> !x&&!y
   *   !(x&&y) --> !x||!y
   *   !!x     --> x
   * Thus:
   *   !(x&&!y) --> !x||!!y --> !x||y
   */
Method code:
1861 |   void tryMinimizeCondition(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
1862 | 
1863 |     switch (n.getType()) {
1864 |       case Token.NOT:
1865 |         Node first = n.getFirstChild();
1866 |         switch (first.getType()) {
1867 |           case Token.NOT: {
1868 |               Node newRoot = first.removeFirstChild();
1869 |               parent.replaceChild(n, newRoot);
1870 |               n = newRoot; // continue from here.
1871 |               t.getCompiler().reportCodeChange();
1872 | 
1873 |               // The child has moved up, to minimize it recurse.
1874 |               tryMinimizeCondition(t, n, parent);
1875 |               return;
1876 |             }
1877 |           case Token.AND:
1878 |           case Token.OR: {
1879 |               Node leftParent = first.getFirstChild();
1880 |               Node rightParent = first.getLastChild();
1881 |               if (leftParent.getType() != Token.NOT
1882 |                   || rightParent.getType() != Token.NOT) {
1883 |                 // No NOTs to elminate.
1884 |                 break;
1885 |               }
1886 |               Node left = leftParent.removeFirstChild();
1887 |               Node right = rightParent.removeFirstChild();
1888 | 
1889 |               int newOp = (first.getType() == Token.AND) ? Token.OR : Token.AND;
1890 |               Node newRoot = new Node(newOp, left, right);
1891 |               parent.replaceChild(n, newRoot);
1892 |               n = newRoot; // continue from here.
1893 |               t.getCompiler().reportCodeChange();
1894 | 
1895 |               // Unlike the NOT case above, we know that AND and OR are
1896 |               // valid root to check minimize so just break out and check
1897 |               // the children.
1898 |             }
1899 |             break;
1900 |         }
1901 |         break;
1902 | 
1903 |       case Token.OR:
1904 |       case Token.AND:
1905 |         // check the children.
1906 |         break;
1907 | 
1908 |       default:
1909 |         // if(true) --> if(1)
1910 |         if (NodeUtil.isLiteralValue(n)) {
1911 |           boolean result = NodeUtil.getBooleanValue(n);
1912 |           int equivalentResult = result ? 1 : 0;
1913 |           maybeReplaceChildWithNumber(t, n, parent, equivalentResult);
1914 |         }
1915 |         // We can't do anything else currently.
1916 |         return;
1917 |     }
1918 | 
1919 |     for (Node c = n.getFirstChild(); c != null; ) {
1920 |       Node next = c.getNext();  // c may be removed.
1921 |       tryMinimizeCondition(t, c, n);
1922 |       c = next;
1923 |     }
1924 |   }

===== M0329 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.JSTypeRegistry.registerPropertyOnType(String,ObjectType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/JSTypeRegistry.java
Original range: 524-531
Documentation:
/**
   * Tells the type system that {@code owner} may have a property named
   * {@code propertyName}. This allows the registry to keep track of what
   * types a property is defined upon.
   *
   * This is NOT the same as saying that {@code owner} must have a property
   * named type. ObjectType#hasProperty attempts to minimize false positives
   * ("if we're not sure, then don't type check this property"). The type
   * registry, on the other hand, should attempt to minimize false negatives
   * ("if this property is assigned anywhere in the program, it must
   * show up in the type registry").
   */
Method code:
524 |   public void registerPropertyOnType(String propertyName, ObjectType owner) {
525 |     Set<ObjectType> typeSet = typesIndexedByProperty.get(propertyName);
526 |     if (typeSet == null) {
527 |       typesIndexedByProperty.put(propertyName, typeSet = Sets.newHashSet());
528 |     }
529 |     greatestSubtypeByProperty.remove(propertyName);
530 |     typeSet.add(owner);
531 |   }

===== M0330 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.FoldConstants.tryFoldLeftChildAdd(NodeTraversal,Node,Node,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/FoldConstants.java
Original range: 1017-1043
Documentation:
/**
   * Expressions such as [foo() + 'a' + 'b'] generate parse trees
   * where no node has two const children ((foo() + 'a') + 'b'), so
   * tryFoldAdd() won't fold it -- tryFoldLeftChildAdd() will (for Strings).
   * Specifically it folds Add exprssions where:
   *  - The left child is also and add expression
   *  - The right child is a constant value
   *  - The left child's right child is a STRING constant.
   *
   * WARNING: If javascript ever adds operator overloading, this will
   * probably stop being correct.
   */
Method code:
1017 |   void tryFoldLeftChildAdd(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node left, Node right,
1018 |                            Node parent) {
1019 | 
1020 |     if (NodeUtil.isLiteralValue(right) &&
1021 |         left.getType() == Token.ADD &&
1022 |         left.getChildCount() == 2) {
1023 | 
1024 |       Node ll = left.getFirstChild();
1025 |       Node lr = ll.getNext();
1026 | 
1027 |       // Left's right child MUST be a string. We would not want to fold
1028 |       // foo() + 2 + 'a' because we don't know what foo() will return, and
1029 |       // therefore we don't know if left is a string concat, or a numeric add.
1030 |       if (lr.getType() != Token.STRING)
1031 |         return;
1032 | 
1033 |       String leftString = NodeUtil.getStringValue(lr);
1034 |       String rightString = NodeUtil.getStringValue(right);
1035 |       if (leftString != null && rightString != null) {
1036 |         left.removeChild(ll);
1037 |         String result = leftString + rightString;
1038 |         n.replaceChild(left, ll);
1039 |         n.replaceChild(right, Node.newString(result));
1040 |         t.getCompiler().reportCodeChange();
1041 |       }
1042 |     }
1043 |   }

===== M0335 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.FoldConstants.tryFoldAssign(NodeTraversal,Node,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/FoldConstants.java
Original range: 412-473
Documentation:

Method code:
412 |   private void tryFoldAssign(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node left, Node right) {
413 |     Preconditions.checkArgument(n.getType() == Token.ASSIGN);
414 | 
415 |     // Tries to convert x = x + y -> x += y;
416 |     if (!right.hasChildren() ||
417 |         right.getFirstChild().getNext() != right.getLastChild()) {
418 |       // RHS must have two children.
419 |       return;
420 |     }
421 | 
422 |     if (NodeUtil.mayHaveSideEffects(left)) {
423 |       return;
424 |     }
425 | 
426 |     Node leftChild = right.getFirstChild();
427 |     if (!compiler.areNodesEqualForInlining(left, leftChild)) {
428 |       return;
429 |     }
430 | 
431 |     int newType = -1;
432 |     switch (right.getType()) {
433 |       case Token.ADD:
434 |         newType = Token.ASSIGN_ADD;
435 |         break;
436 |       case Token.BITAND:
437 |         newType = Token.ASSIGN_BITAND;
438 |         break;
439 |       case Token.BITOR:
440 |         newType = Token.ASSIGN_BITOR;
441 |         break;
442 |       case Token.BITXOR:
443 |         newType = Token.ASSIGN_BITXOR;
444 |         break;
445 |       case Token.DIV:
446 |         newType = Token.ASSIGN_DIV;
447 |         break;
448 |       case Token.LSH:
449 |         newType = Token.ASSIGN_LSH;
450 |         break;
451 |       case Token.MOD:
452 |         newType = Token.ASSIGN_MOD;
453 |         break;
454 |       case Token.MUL:
455 |         newType = Token.ASSIGN_MUL;
456 |         break;
457 |       case Token.RSH:
458 |         newType = Token.ASSIGN_RSH;
459 |         break;
460 |       case Token.SUB:
461 |         newType = Token.ASSIGN_SUB;
462 |         break;
463 |       case Token.URSH:
464 |         newType = Token.ASSIGN_URSH;
465 |         break;
466 |       default:
467 |         return;
468 |     }
469 | 
470 |     n.getParent().replaceChild(n, new Node(newType,
471 |         left.detachFromParent(), right.getLastChild().detachFromParent()));
472 |     t.getCompiler().reportCodeChange();
473 |   }

/**
   * Add variable arguments to the end of the parameter list.
   * @return False if this is called after var args are added.
   */
Method code:
94 |   public boolean addVarArgs(JSType type) {
95 |     if (hasVarArgs()) {
96 |       return false;
97 |     }
98 | 
99 |     // There are two types of variable argument functions:
100 |     // 1) Programmer-defined var args
101 |     // 2) Native bottom types that can accept any argument.
102 |     // For the first one, "undefined" is a valid value for all arguments.
103 |     // For the second, we do not want to cast it up to undefined.
104 |     if (!type.isEmptyType()) {
105 |       type = registry.createOptionalType(type);
106 |     }
107 |     newParameter(type).setVarArgs(true);
108 |     return true;
109 |   }

===== M0339 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.GoogleCodingConvention.isConstant(String)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/GoogleCodingConvention.java
Original range: 59-81
Documentation:
/**
   * {@inheritDoc}
   *
   * <p>This enforces the Google const name convention, that the first character
   * after the last $ must be an upper-case letter and all subsequent letters
   * must be upper case. The name must be at least 2 characters long.
   *
   * <p>Examples:
   * <pre>
   *      aaa          Not constant - lower-case letters in the name
   *      A            Not constant - too short
   *      goog$A       Constant - letters after the $ are upper-case.
   *      AA17         Constant - digits can appear after the first letter
   *      goog$7A      Not constant - first character after the $ must be
   *                   upper case.
   *      $A           Constant - doesn't have to be anything in front of the $
   * </pre>
   */
Method code:
59 |   @Override
60 |   public boolean isConstant(String name) {
61 |     if (name.length() <= 1) {
62 |       return false;
63 |     }
64 | 
65 |     // In compiled code, '$' is often a namespace delimiter. To allow inlining
66 |     // of namespaced constants, we strip off any namespaces here.
67 |     int pos = name.lastIndexOf('$');
68 |     if (pos >= 0) {
69 |       name = name.substring(pos + 1);
70 |       if (name.length() == 0) {
71 |         return false;
72 |       }
73 |     }
74 | 
75 |     if (!Character.isUpperCase(name.charAt(0))) {
76 |       return false;
77 |     }
78 | 
79 |     // hack way of checking that there aren't any lower-case letters
80 |     return name.toUpperCase().equals(name);
81 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.IRFactory.TransformDispatcher.transformAsString(AstNode)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/IRFactory.java
Original range: 315-323
Documentation:
/**
     * Transforms the given node and then sets its type to Token.STRING if it
     * was Token.NAME. If its type was already Token.STRING, then quotes it.
     * Used for properties, as the old AST uses String tokens, while the new one
     * uses Name tokens for unquoted strings. For example, in
     * var o = {'a' : 1, b: 2};
     * the string 'a' is quoted, while the name b is turned into a string, but
     * unquoted.
     */
Method code:
315 |     private Node transformAsString(AstNode n) {
316 |       Node ret = transform(n);
317 |       if (ret.getType() == Token.STRING) {
318 |         ret.putBooleanProp(Node.QUOTED_PROP, true);
319 |       } else if (ret.getType() == Token.NAME) {
320 |         ret.setType(Token.STRING);
321 |       }
322 |       return ret;
323 |     }

Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.JSTypeRegistry.createParameters(boolean,JSType...)

Original range: 973-984
Documentation:
/**
   * Creates a tree hierarchy representing a typed argument list.
   *
   * @param lastVarArgs whether the last type should considered as a variable
   *     length argument.
   * @param parameterTypes the parameter types. The last element of this array
   *     is considered a variable length argument is {@code lastVarArgs} is
   *     {@code true}.
   * @return a tree hierarchy representing a typed argument list
   */
Method code:
973 |   private Node createParameters(boolean lastVarArgs, JSType... parameterTypes) {
974 |     FunctionParamBuilder builder = new FunctionParamBuilder(this);
975 |     int max = parameterTypes.length - 1;
976 |     for (int i = 0; i <= max; i++) {
977 |       if (lastVarArgs && i == max) {
978 |         builder.addVarArgs(parameterTypes[i]);
979 |       } else {
980 |         builder.addRequiredParams(parameterTypes[i]);
981 |       }
982 |     }
983 |     return builder.build();
984 |   }

===== M0358 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CodingConventionAnnotator.visit(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CodingConventionAnnotator.java
Original range: 55-106
Documentation:

Method code:
55 |   @Override
56 |   public void visit(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
57 |     int nType = n.getType();
58 |     switch (nType) {
59 |       case Token.NAME:
60 |       case Token.STRING:
61 |         String nString = n.getString();
62 |         if (nType == Token.NAME &&
63 |             n.getParent().getType() == Token.CALL &&
64 |             "eval".equals(nString)) {
65 |           n.putBooleanProp(Node.DIRECT_EVAL, true);
66 |         }
67 |         if (convention.isConstant(nString)) {
68 |           n.putBooleanProp(Node.IS_CONSTANT_NAME, true);
69 |         }
70 |         break;
71 | 
72 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
73 |         JSDocInfo fnInfo = n.getJSDocInfo();
74 |         if (fnInfo == null) {
75 |           // Look for the info on other nodes.
76 |           if (parent.getType() == Token.ASSIGN) {
77 |             // on ASSIGNs
78 |             fnInfo = parent.getJSDocInfo();
79 |           } else if (parent.getType() == Token.NAME) {
80 |             // on var NAME = function() { ... };
81 |             fnInfo = parent.getParent().getJSDocInfo();
82 |           }
83 |         }
84 | 
85 |         // Compute which function parameters are optional and
86 |         // which are var_args.
87 |         Node args = n.getFirstChild().getNext();
88 |         for (Node arg = args.getFirstChild();
89 |              arg != null;
90 |              arg = arg.getNext()) {
91 |           String argName = arg.getString();
92 |           JSTypeExpression typeExpr = fnInfo == null ?
93 |               null : fnInfo.getParameterType(argName);
94 | 
95 |           if (convention.isOptionalParameter(arg) ||
96 |               typeExpr != null && typeExpr.isOptionalArg()) {
97 |             arg.putBooleanProp(Node.IS_OPTIONAL_PARAM, true);
98 |           }
99 |           if (convention.isVarArgsParameter(arg) ||
100 |               typeExpr != null && typeExpr.isVarArgs()) {
101 |             arg.putBooleanProp(Node.IS_VAR_ARGS_PARAM, true);
102 |           }
103 |         }
104 |         break;
105 |     }
106 |   }

===== M0362 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CodeConsumer.addOp(String,boolean)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CodeConsumer.java
Original range: 232-260
Documentation:

Method code:
232 |   void addOp(String op, boolean binOp) {
233 |     maybeEndStatement();
234 | 
235 |     char first = op.charAt(0);
236 |     char prev = getLastChar();
237 | 
238 |     if ((first == '+' || first == '-') && prev == first) {
239 |       // This is not pretty printing. This is to prevent misparsing of
240 |       // things like "x + ++y" or "x++ + ++y"
241 |       append(" ");
242 |     } else if (Character.isLetter(first) &&
243 |                isWordChar(prev)) {
244 |       // Make sure there is a space after e.g. instanceof , typeof
245 |       append(" ");
246 |     } else if (prev == '-' && first == '>') {
247 |       // Make sure that we don't emit -->
248 |       append(" ");
249 |     }
250 | 
251 |     // Allow formating around the operator.
252 |     appendOp(op, binOp);
253 | 
254 |     // Line breaking after an operator is always safe. Line breaking before an
255 |     // operator on the other hand is not. We only line break after a bin op
256 |     // because it looks strange.
257 |     if (binOp) {
258 |       maybeCutLine();
259 |     }
260 |   }

===== M0364 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.isImmutableValue(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 176-197
Documentation:
/**
   * Returns true if this is an immutable value.
   */
Method code:
176 |   static boolean isImmutableValue(Node n) {
177 |     switch (n.getType()) {
178 |       case Token.STRING:
179 |       case Token.NUMBER:
180 |       case Token.NULL:
181 |       case Token.TRUE:
182 |       case Token.FALSE:
183 |       case Token.VOID:
184 |         return true;
185 |       case Token.NEG:
186 |         return isImmutableValue(n.getFirstChild());
187 |       case Token.NAME:
188 |         String name = n.getString();
189 |         // We assume here that programs don't change the value of the keyword
190 |         // undefined to something other than the value undefined.
191 |         return "undefined".equals(name)
192 |             || "Infinity".equals(name)
193 |             || "NaN".equals(name);
194 |     }
195 | 
196 |     return false;
197 |   }

===== M0371 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.UnionTypeBuilder.build()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/UnionTypeBuilder.java
Original range: 136-163
Documentation:
/**
   * Creates a union.
   * @return A UnionType if it has two or more alternates, the
   *    only alternate if it has one and otherwise {@code NO_TYPE}.
   */
Method code:
136 |   JSType build() {
137 |     if (result == null) {
138 |       if (isAllType) {
139 |         result = registry.getNativeType(ALL_TYPE);
140 |       } else if (isNativeUnknownType) {
141 |         if (areAllUnknownsChecked) {
142 |           result = registry.getNativeType(CHECKED_UNKNOWN_TYPE);
143 |         } else {
144 |           result = registry.getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE);
145 |         }
146 |       } else {
147 |         Set<JSType> alternateSet = Sets.newUnmodifiableHashSet(alternates);
148 |         int size = alternateSet.size();
149 |         if (size > MAX_UNION_SIZE) {
150 |           result = registry.getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE);
151 |         } else {
152 |           if (size > 1) {
153 |             result = new UnionType(registry, alternateSet);
154 |           } else if (size == 1) {
155 |             result = alternates.iterator().next();
156 |           } else {
157 |             result = registry.getNativeType(NO_TYPE);
158 |           }
159 |         }
160 |       }
161 |     }
162 |     return result;
163 |   }

===== M0374 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CodePrinter.CompactCodePrinter.maybeCutLine()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CodePrinter.java
Original range: 454-470
Documentation:
/**
     * This may start a new line if the current line is longer than the line
     * length threshold.
     */
Method code:
454 |     @Override
455 |     void maybeCutLine() {
456 |       if (lineLength > lineLengthThreshold) {
457 |         // Use the preferred position provided it will break the line.
458 |         if (preferredBreakPosition > lineStartPosition &&
459 |             preferredBreakPosition < lineStartPosition + lineLength) {
460 |           int position = preferredBreakPosition;
461 |           code.insert(position, '\n');
462 |           reportLineCut(lineIndex, position - lineStartPosition);
463 |           lineIndex++;
464 |           lineLength -= (position - lineStartPosition);
465 |           lineStartPosition = position + 1;
466 |         } else {
467 |           startNewLine();
468 |         }
469 |       }
470 |     }

Documentation:

Method code:
189 |   @Override
190 |   public int hashCode() {
191 |     int hashCode = 0;
192 |     if (returnType != null) {
193 |       hashCode += returnType.hashCode();
194 |     }
195 |     if (parameters != null) {
196 |       Node param = parameters.getFirstChild();
197 |       while (param != null) {
198 |         JSType paramType = param.getJSType();
199 |         if (paramType != null) {
200 |           hashCode += paramType.hashCode();
201 |         }
202 |         param = param.getNext();
203 |       }
204 |     }
205 |     return hashCode;
206 |   }

===== M0385 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.isLatin(String)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 1604-1614
Documentation:
/** Test if all characters in the string are in the Basic Latin (aka ASCII)
   * character set - that they have UTF-16 values equal to or below 0x7f.
   * This check can find which identifiers with Unicode characters need to be
   * escaped in order to allow resulting files to be processed by non-Unicode
   * aware UNIX tools and editors.
   * *
   * See http://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Latin_characters_in_Unicode
   * for more on Basic Latin.
   *
   * @param s The string to be checked for ASCII-goodness.
   *
   * @return True if all characters in the string are in Basic Latin set.
   */
Method code:
1604 |   static boolean isLatin(String s) {
1605 |     char LARGEST_BASIC_LATIN = 0x7f;
1606 |     int len = s.length();
1607 |     for (int index = 0; index < len; index++) {
1608 |       char c = s.charAt(index);
1609 |       if (c > LARGEST_BASIC_LATIN) {
1610 |         return false;
1611 |       }
1612 |     }
1613 |     return true;
1614 |   }

===== M0388 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.Node.isEquivalentTo(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/Node.java
Original range: 1543-1577
Documentation:
/** Returns true if this node is equivalent semantically to another */
Method code:
1543 |     public boolean isEquivalentTo(Node node) {
1544 |         if (type == Token.ARRAYLIT) {
1545 |             try {
1546 |                 int[] indices1 = (int[])getProp(Node.SKIP_INDEXES_PROP);
1547 |                 int[] indices2 = (int[])node.getProp(Node.SKIP_INDEXES_PROP);
1548 |                 if (indices1 == null) {
1549 |                     if (indices2 != null)
1550 |                         return false;
1551 |                 } else if (indices2 == null) {
1552 |                     return false;
1553 |                 } else if (indices1.length != indices2.length) {
1554 |                     return false;
1555 |                 } else {
1556 |                     for (int i = 0; i < indices1.length; i++) {
1557 |                         if (indices1[i] != indices2[i])
1558 |                             return false;
1559 |                     }
1560 |                 }
1561 |             } catch (Exception e) {
1562 |                 return false;
1563 |             }
1564 |         } else if (type == Token.INC ||
1565 |                    type == Token.DEC) {
1566 |             int post1 = this.getIntProp(INCRDECR_PROP, 0);
1567 |             int post2 = node.getIntProp(INCRDECR_PROP, 0);
1568 |             if (post1 != post2)
1569 |                 return false;
1570 |         } else if (type == Token.STRING) {
1571 |             int quoted1 = this.getIntProp(QUOTED_PROP, 0);
1572 |             int quoted2 = node.getIntProp(QUOTED_PROP, 0);
1573 |             if (quoted1 != quoted2)
1574 |                 return false;
1575 |         }
1576 |         return true;
1577 |     }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.precedence(int)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 627-702
Documentation:
/*
   *  0 comma ,
   *  1 assignment = += -= *= /= %= <<= >>= >>>= &= ^= |=
   *  2 conditional ?:
   *  3 logical-or ||
   *  4 logical-and &&
   *  5 bitwise-or |
   *  6 bitwise-xor ^
   *  7 bitwise-and &
   *  8 equality == !=
   *  9 relational < <= > >=
   * 10 bitwise shift << >> >>>
   * 11 addition/subtraction + -
   * 12 multiply/divide * / %
   * 13 negation/increment ! ~ - ++ --
   * 14 call, member () [] .
   */
Method code:
627 |   static int precedence(int type) {
628 |     switch (type) {
629 |       case Token.COMMA:  return 0;
630 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITOR:
631 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITXOR:
632 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITAND:
633 |       case Token.ASSIGN_LSH:
634 |       case Token.ASSIGN_RSH:
635 |       case Token.ASSIGN_URSH:
636 |       case Token.ASSIGN_ADD:
637 |       case Token.ASSIGN_SUB:
638 |       case Token.ASSIGN_MUL:
639 |       case Token.ASSIGN_DIV:
640 |       case Token.ASSIGN_MOD:
641 |       case Token.ASSIGN: return 1;
642 |       case Token.HOOK:   return 2;  // ?: operator
643 |       case Token.OR:     return 3;
644 |       case Token.AND:    return 4;
645 |       case Token.BITOR:  return 5;
646 |       case Token.BITXOR: return 6;
647 |       case Token.BITAND: return 7;
648 |       case Token.EQ:
649 |       case Token.NE:
650 |       case Token.SHEQ:
651 |       case Token.SHNE:   return 8;
652 |       case Token.LT:
653 |       case Token.GT:
654 |       case Token.LE:
655 |       case Token.GE:
656 |       case Token.INSTANCEOF:
657 |       case Token.IN:     return 9;
658 |       case Token.LSH:
659 |       case Token.RSH:
660 |       case Token.URSH:   return 10;
661 |       case Token.SUB:
662 |       case Token.ADD:    return 11;
663 |       case Token.MUL:
664 |       case Token.MOD:
665 |       case Token.DIV:    return 12;
666 |       case Token.INC:
667 |       case Token.DEC:
668 |       case Token.NEW:
669 |       case Token.DELPROP:
670 |       case Token.TYPEOF:
671 |       case Token.VOID:
672 |       case Token.NOT:
673 |       case Token.BITNOT:
674 |       case Token.POS:
675 |       case Token.NEG:    return 13;
676 | 
677 |       case Token.ARRAYLIT:
678 |       case Token.CALL:
679 |       case Token.EMPTY:
680 |       case Token.FALSE:
681 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
682 |       case Token.GETELEM:
683 |       case Token.GETPROP:
684 |       case Token.GET_REF:
685 |       case Token.IF:
686 |       case Token.LP:
687 |       case Token.NAME:
688 |       case Token.NULL:
689 |       case Token.NUMBER:
690 |       case Token.OBJECTLIT:
691 |       case Token.REGEXP:
692 |       case Token.RETURN:
693 |       case Token.STRING:
694 |       case Token.THIS:
695 |       case Token.TRUE:
696 |         return 15;
697 | 
698 |       default: throw new Error("Unknown precedence for " +
699 |                                Node.tokenToName(type) +
700 |                                " (type " + type + ")");
701 |     }
702 |   }

===== M0395 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeTypeNormalizer.normalizeJsDocAnnotations(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeTypeNormalizer.java
Original range: 68-97
Documentation:
/**
   * Normalize where JSDoc annotations appear on the AST.
   *
   * In the AST that Rhino gives us, it needs to make a distinction
   * between jsdoc on the object literal node and jsdoc on the object literal
   * value. For example,
   * <pre>
   * var x = {
   *   / JSDOC /
   *   a: 'b',
   *   c: / JSDOC / 'd'
   * };
   * </pre>
   *
   * But in few narrow cases (in particular, function literals), it's
   * a lot easier for us if the doc is attached to the value.
   */
Method code:
68 |   private void normalizeJsDocAnnotations(Node n) {
69 |     if (n.getType() == Token.OBJECTLIT) {
70 |       for (Node key = n.getFirstChild();
71 |            key != null; key = key.getNext().getNext()) {
72 |         Node value = key.getNext();
73 |         if (key.getJSDocInfo() != null &&
74 |             key.getNext().getType() == Token.FUNCTION) {
75 |           value.setJSDocInfo(key.getJSDocInfo());
76 |         }
77 |       }
78 |     }
79 | 
80 |     // TODO(johnlenz): Determine if it is possible to simply use the javadoc
81 |     // everywhere rather than use IS_DISPATCHER.
82 |     /*
83 |      * Translate dispatcher info into the property expected node.
84 |      */
85 |     if (n.getJSDocInfo() != null && n.getJSDocInfo().isJavaDispatch()) {
86 |       if (n.getType() == Token.ASSIGN) {
87 |         Node fnNode = n.getLastChild();
88 |         Preconditions.checkState(fnNode.getType() == Token.FUNCTION);
89 |         fnNode.putBooleanProp(Node.IS_DISPATCHER, true);
90 |       }
91 |     }
92 | 
93 |     for (Node child = n.getFirstChild();
94 |          child != null; child = child.getNext()) {
95 |       normalizeJsDocAnnotations(child);
96 |     }
97 |   }

===== M0396 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.IRFactory.TransformDispatcher.processArrayLiteral(ArrayLiteral)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/IRFactory.java
Original range: 325-356
Documentation:

Method code:
325 |     @Override
326 |     Node processArrayLiteral(ArrayLiteral literalNode) {
327 |       if (literalNode.isDestructuring()) {
328 |         reportDestructuringAssign(literalNode);
329 |       }
330 | 
331 |       Node node = new Node(Token.ARRAYLIT);
332 |       int skipCount = 0;
333 |       for (AstNode child : literalNode.getElements()) {
334 |         Node c = transform(child);
335 |         if (c.getType() == Token.EMPTY) {
336 |           skipCount++;
337 |         }
338 |         node.addChildToBack(c);
339 | 
340 |       }
341 |       if (skipCount > 0) {
342 |         int[] skipIndexes = new int[skipCount];
343 |         int i = 0;
344 |         int j = 0;
345 |         for (Node child : node.children()) {
346 |           if (child.getType() == Token.EMPTY) {
347 |             node.removeChild(child);
348 |             skipIndexes[j] = i;
349 |             j++;
350 |           }
351 |           i++;
352 |         }
353 |         node.putProp(Node.SKIP_INDEXES_PROP, skipIndexes);
354 |       }
355 |       return node;
356 |     }

58 |     } else {

62 |       }

===== M0408 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CodeGenerator.jsString(String,CharsetEncoder)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CodeGenerator.java
Original range: 777-804
Documentation:
/** Outputs a js string, using the optimal (single/double) quote character */
Method code:
777 |   static String jsString(String s, CharsetEncoder outputCharsetEncoder) {
778 |     int singleq = 0, doubleq = 0;
779 | 
780 |     // could count the quotes and pick the optimal quote character
781 |     for (int i = 0; i < s.length(); i++) {
782 |       switch (s.charAt(i)) {
783 |         case '"': doubleq++; break;
784 |         case '\'': singleq++; break;
785 |       }
786 |     }
787 | 
788 |     String doublequote, singlequote;
789 |     char quote;
790 |     if (singleq < doubleq) {
791 |       // more double quotes so escape the single quotes
792 |       quote = '\'';
793 |       doublequote = "\"";
794 |       singlequote = "\\\'";
795 |     } else {
796 |       // more single quotes so escape the doubles
797 |       quote = '\"';
798 |       doublequote = "\\\"";
799 |       singlequote = "\'";
800 |     }
801 | 
802 |     return strEscape(s, quote, doublequote, singlequote, "\\\\",
803 |         outputCharsetEncoder);
804 |   }

===== M0411 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.getStringValue(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 109-136
Documentation:
/**
   * Gets the value of a node as a String, or null if it cannot be converted.
   * When it returns a non-null String, this method effectively emulates the
   * <code>String()</code> JavaScript cast function.
   */
Method code:
109 |   static String getStringValue(Node n) {
110 |     // TODO(user): Convert constant array, object, and regex literals as well.
111 |     switch (n.getType()) {
112 |       case Token.NAME:
113 |       case Token.STRING:
114 |         return n.getString();
115 | 
116 |       case Token.NUMBER:
117 |         double value = n.getDouble();
118 |         long longValue = (long) value;
119 | 
120 |         // Return "1" instead of "1.0"
121 |         if (longValue == value) {
122 |           return Long.toString(longValue);
123 |         } else {
124 |           return Double.toString(n.getDouble());
125 |         }
126 | 
127 |       case Token.FALSE:
128 |       case Token.TRUE:
129 |       case Token.NULL:
130 |         return Node.tokenToName(n.getType());
131 | 
132 |       case Token.VOID:
133 |         return "undefined";
134 |     }
135 |     return null;
136 |   }

===== M0413 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.IRFactory.transformTree(AstRoot,String,Config,ErrorReporter)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/IRFactory.java
Original range: 109-156
Documentation:

Method code:
109 |   public static Node transformTree(AstRoot node,
110 |                                    String sourceString,
111 |                                    Config config,
112 |                                    ErrorReporter errorReporter) {
113 |     IRFactory irFactory = new IRFactory(sourceString, node.getSourceName(),
114 |         config, errorReporter);
115 |     Node irNode = irFactory.transform(node);
116 |     // @license text gets appended onto the fileLevelJsDocBuilder as found,
117 |     // and stored straight into the JSDocInfo for the root node.
118 |     Node.FileLevelJsDocBuilder fileLevelJsDocBuilder =
119 |         irNode.getJsDocBuilderForNode();
120 |     // fileOverviewInfo stores the last bit of fileoverview data we saw.
121 |     // We only permit one, so throwing away extras is fair.
122 |     // The fileOverviewInfo gets passed into parseJSDocInfo so that
123 |     // it can detect when multiple @fileoverviews exist in the same file.
124 |     JSDocInfo fileOverviewInfo = null;
125 |     if (node.getComments() != null) {
126 |       for (Comment comment : node.getComments()) {
127 |         if (comment.getCommentType() == JSDOC &&
128 |             (comment.getValue().contains("@fileoverview") ||
129 |              comment.getValue().contains("@preserve") ||
130 |              comment.getValue().contains("@license"))) {
131 |           JSDocInfo info = irFactory.parseJSDocInfo(comment.getValue(),
132 |               comment.getLineno(), comment.getAbsolutePosition(),
133 |               fileLevelJsDocBuilder, fileOverviewInfo);
134 |           if (info != null && fileOverviewInfo == null) {
135 |             fileOverviewInfo = info;
136 |           }
137 |         }
138 |       }
139 | 
140 |       // Only after we've seen all @fileoverview entries, attach the
141 |       // last one to the root node, and copy the found license strings
142 |       // to that node.
143 |       if (fileOverviewInfo != null) {
144 |         if ((irNode.getJSDocInfo() != null) &&
145 |             (irNode.getJSDocInfo().getLicense() != null)) {
146 |           fileOverviewInfo.setLicense(irNode.getJSDocInfo().getLicense());
147 |         }
148 |         irNode.setJSDocInfo(fileOverviewInfo);
149 |       }
150 | 
151 |       Comment[] comments = new Comment[node.getComments().size()];
152 |       comments = node.getComments().toArray(comments);
153 |       irFactory.parseAllJsDocInfo(irNode, comments, 0);
154 |     }
155 |     return irNode;
156 |   }
